#![allow(non_camel_case_types)]
#![allow(unused_parens)]
#![allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
#![allow(clippy::unnecessary_cast)]


// =============== Constants and Enums ===============







const MZ_UINT32_MAX: u64 = 0xFFFFFFFFu64;
const MZ_UINT16_MAX: u32 = 0xFFFFu32;

// =============== Data Structures ===============

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub enum ZipType {
    Invalid,
    User,
    Memory,
    File,
    CFile,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct ZipArray<T> {
    data: Vec<T>,
}

impl<T> ZipArray<T> {
    fn new() -> Self {
        Self { data: Vec::new() }
    }

    fn resize(&mut self, new_size: usize) -> bool {
        if new_size > self.data.capacity() {
            self.data.reserve(new_size - self.data.len());
        }
        if new_size > self.data.len() {
            self.data.resize(new_size, unsafe { zeroed() });
            true
        } else {
            self.data.truncate(new_size);
            true
        }
    }

    fn clear(&mut self) {
        self.data.clear();
    }

    fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.data.len()
    }

    fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.data.is_empty()
    }

    fn as_ptr(&self) -> *const T {
        self.data.as_ptr()
    }

    fn as_mut_ptr(&mut self) -> *mut T {
        self.data.as_mut_ptr()
    }

    fn get(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&T> {
        self.data.get(index)
    }

    fn get_mut(&mut self, index: usize) -> Option<&mut T> {
        self.data.get_mut(index)
    }
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct ZipInternalState {
    central_dir: ZipArray<u8>,
    central_dir_offsets: ZipArray<u32>,
    sorted_central_dir_offsets: ZipArray<u32>,
    init_flags: u32,
    zip64: bool,
    zip64_has_extended_info_fields: bool,
    p_file: Option<File>,
    file_archive_start_ofs: u64,
    p_mem: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    mem_size: usize,
    mem_capacity: usize,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct ZipArchive {
    pub m_archive_size: u64,
    pub m_total_files: u32,
    pub m_central_directory_file_ofs: u64,
    pub m_zip_type: ZipType,
    pub m_p_state: Option<Box<ZipInternalState>>,
    pub m_last_error: ZipError,
    pub m_zip_mode: ZipMode,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub enum ZipMode {
    Invalid,
    Reading,
    Writing,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub enum ZipError {
    Ok,
    InvalidParameter,
    NotAnArchive,
    FailedFindingCentralDir,
    FileReadFailed,
    UnsupportedMultidisk,
    TooManyFiles,
    UnsupportedCdirSize,
    InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted,
    AllocFailed,
    UnsupportedEncryption,
    UnsupportedMethod,
    UnsupportedFeature,
    FileOpenFailed,
    FileSeekFailed,
    FileCloseFailed,
    Other(u32),
}

impl ZipArchive {
    fn set_error(&mut self, error: ZipError) -> bool {
        self.m_last_error = error;
        false
    }

    fn reader_init_internal(&mut self, flags: u32) -> bool {
        self.m_p_state = Some(Box::new(ZipInternalState {
            central_dir: ZipArray::new(),
            central_dir_offsets: ZipArray::new(),
            sorted_central_dir_offsets: ZipArray::new(),
            init_flags: flags,
            zip64: false,
            zip64_has_extended_info_fields: false,
            p_file: None,
            file_archive_start_ofs: 0,
            p_mem: None,
            mem_size: 0,
            mem_capacity: 0,
        }));
        self.m_zip_mode = ZipMode::Reading;
        true
    }

    fn locate_header_sig(&self, sig: u32, header_size: usize, p_file_ofs: &mut i64) -> bool {
        // Simplified implementation - actual implementation would search backwards
        // from end of archive for the signature
        *p_file_ofs = self.m_archive_size as i64 - header_size as i64;
        true
    }

    fn eocd64_valid(&self, ofs: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> bool {
        if buf.len() < MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE {
            return false;
        }
        let sig = u32::from_le_bytes([buf[0], buf[1], buf[2], buf[3]]);
        sig == MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG
    }

    fn sort_central_dir_offsets_by_filename(&mut self) {
        // Simplified implementation - actual implementation would sort
        // m_sorted_central_dir_offsets based on lowercase filenames
    }
}

// =============== Helper Functions ===============

fn read_le16(data: &[u8]) -> u16 {
    u16::from_le_bytes([data[0], data[1]])
}

fn read_le32(data: &[u8]) -> u32 {
    u32::from_le_bytes([data[0], data[1], data[2], data[3]])
}

fn read_le64(data: &[u8]) -> u64 {
    u64::from_le_bytes([
        data[0], data[1], data[2], data[3], data[4], data[5], data[6], data[7],
    ])
}

// =============== Main Functions ===============

fn mz_zip_reader_read_central_dir(p_zip: &mut ZipArchive, flags: u32) -> bool {
    let mut cdir_size: u32 = 0;
    let mut cdir_entries_on_this_disk: u32 = 0;
    let mut num_this_disk: u32 = 0;
    let mut cdir_disk_index: u32 = 0;
    let mut cdir_ofs: u64 = 0;
    let mut eocd_ofs: u64 = 0;
    let mut archive_ofs: u64 = 0;
    let mut cur_file_ofs: i64 = 0;

    let mut buf_u32 = [0u32; 4096 / 4];
    let p_buf = unsafe {
        slice::from_raw_parts_mut(
            buf_u32.as_mut_ptr() as *mut u8,
            buf_u32.len() * size_of::<u32>(),
        )
    };

    let sort_central_dir = (flags & 0x800) == 0; // MZ_ZIP_FLAG_DO_NOT_SORT_CENTRAL_DIRECTORY

    let mut zip64_locator_buf = [0u8; MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE];
    let mut zip64_eocd_buf = [0u8; MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
    let mut zip64_end_of_central_dir_ofs: u64 = 0;

    // Basic sanity checks
    if p_zip.m_archive_size < MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 {
        return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::NotAnArchive);
    }

    if !p_zip.locate_header_sig(
        MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG,
        MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE,
        &mut cur_file_ofs,
    ) {
        return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::FailedFindingCentralDir);
    }

    eocd_ofs = cur_file_ofs as u64;

    // Simplified read - actual implementation would use p_zip.m_pRead
    if cur_file_ofs < 0 || (cur_file_ofs as u64 + MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64) > p_zip.m_archive_size {
        return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
    }

    // Check for zip64 locator
    if let Some(state) = &mut p_zip.m_p_state {
        if cur_file_ofs >= (MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE + MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE) as i64 {
            // Simplified - actual would read from file
            let locator_sig = u32::from_le_bytes([0x50, 0x4b, 0x06, 0x07]); // MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIG
            if locator_sig == MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIG {
                state.zip64 = true;
            }
        }
    }

    // Read EOCD fields
    let sig = read_le32(&p_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_SIG_OFS..]);
    if sig != MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
        return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::NotAnArchive);
    }

    p_zip.m_total_files = read_le16(&p_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_TOTAL_ENTRIES_OFS..]) as u32;
    cdir_entries_on_this_disk = read_le16(&p_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_NUM_ENTRIES_ON_DISK_OFS..]) as u32;
    num_this_disk = read_le16(&p_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_NUM_THIS_DISK_OFS..]) as u32;
    cdir_disk_index = read_le16(&p_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_NUM_DISK_CDIR_OFS..]) as u32;
    cdir_size = read_le32(&p_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_SIZE_OFS..]);
    cdir_ofs = read_le32(&p_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS..]) as u64;

    // Handle zip64 if needed
    if let Some(state) = &p_zip.m_p_state {
        if state.zip64 {
            // Simplified zip64 handling
            let zip64_cdir_total_entries = read_le64(&zip64_eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_TOTAL_ENTRIES_OFS..]);
            if zip64_cdir_total_entries > MZ_UINT32_MAX {
                return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::TooManyFiles);
            }
            p_zip.m_total_files = zip64_cdir_total_entries as u32;
            cdir_ofs = read_le64(&zip64_eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS..]);
        }
    }

    // Validation
    if p_zip.m_total_files != cdir_entries_on_this_disk {
        return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedMultidisk);
    }

    if ((num_this_disk | cdir_disk_index) != 0) && ((num_this_disk != 1) || (cdir_disk_index != 1)) {
        return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedMultidisk);
    }

    if (cdir_ofs + cdir_size as u64) > p_zip.m_archive_size {
        return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }

    p_zip.m_central_directory_file_ofs = cdir_ofs;

    // Read central directory
    if p_zip.m_total_files > 0 {
        if let Some(state) = &mut p_zip.m_p_state {
            if !state.central_dir.resize(cdir_size as usize) {
                return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
            }

            if !state.central_dir_offsets.resize(p_zip.m_total_files as usize) {
                return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
            }

            if sort_central_dir {
                if !state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.resize(p_zip.m_total_files as usize) {
                    return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
                }
            }

            // Simplified read - actual would read from file at cdir_ofs
            // For now, just zero the buffer
            unsafe {
                std::ptr::write_bytes(state.central_dir.as_mut_ptr(), 0, cdir_size as usize);
            }

            // Parse central directory entries
            let mut offset = 0;
            for i in 0..p_zip.m_total_files {
                if offset + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE > cdir_size as usize {
                    return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                }

                let entry_sig = read_le32(&state.central_dir.data[offset..]);
                if entry_sig != MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
                    return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                }

                state.central_dir_offsets.data[i as usize] = offset as u32;
                if sort_central_dir {
                    state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.data[i as usize] = i;
                }

                let bit_flags = read_le16(&state.central_dir.data[offset + MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS..]);
                if (bit_flags & MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_LOCAL_DIR_IS_MASKED) != 0 {
                    return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedEncryption);
                }

                let filename_len = read_le16(&state.central_dir.data[offset + MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
                let extra_len = read_le16(&state.central_dir.data[offset + MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
                let comment_len = read_le16(&state.central_dir.data[offset + MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;

                let total_header_size = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_len + extra_len + comment_len;
                if offset + total_header_size > cdir_size as usize {
                    return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                }

                offset += total_header_size;
            }
        }
    }

    if sort_central_dir {
        p_zip.sort_central_dir_offsets_by_filename();
    }

    true
}

pub fn mz_zip_zero_struct(p_zip: &mut ZipArchive) {
    if let Some(state) = &mut p_zip.m_p_state {
        state.central_dir.clear();
        state.central_dir_offsets.clear();
        state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.clear();
    }
    p_zip.m_archive_size = 0;
    p_zip.m_total_files = 0;
    p_zip.m_central_directory_file_ofs = 0;
    p_zip.m_zip_type = ZipType::Invalid;
    p_zip.m_last_error = ZipError::Ok;
    p_zip.m_zip_mode = ZipMode::Invalid;
}

fn mz_zip_reader_end_internal(p_zip: &mut ZipArchive, set_last_error: bool) -> bool {
    if p_zip.m_p_state.is_none() {
        if set_last_error {
            p_zip.m_last_error = ZipError::InvalidParameter;
        }
        return false;
    }

    if let Some(mut state) = p_zip.m_p_state.take() {
        state.central_dir.clear();
        state.central_dir_offsets.clear();
        state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.clear();

        if let Some(file) = state.p_file.take() {
            if p_zip.m_zip_type == ZipType::File {
                // File closed automatically when dropped
            }
        }
    }

    p_zip.m_zip_mode = ZipMode::Invalid;
    true
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_end(p_zip: &mut ZipArchive) -> bool {
    mz_zip_reader_end_internal(p_zip, true)
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_init(p_zip: &mut ZipArchive, size: u64, flags: u32) -> bool {
    if !p_zip.reader_init_internal(flags) {
        return false;
    }

    p_zip.m_zip_type = ZipType::User;
    p_zip.m_archive_size = size;

    if !mz_zip_reader_read_central_dir(p_zip, flags) {
        mz_zip_reader_end_internal(p_zip, false);
        return false;
    }

    true
}

fn mz_zip_mem_read_func(p_opaque: &ZipArchive, file_ofs: u64, p_buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if let Some(state) = &p_opaque.m_p_state {
        if let Some(mem) = &state.p_mem {
            if file_ofs >= p_opaque.m_archive_size {
                return 0;
            }
            let n = min(p_buf.len(), (p_opaque.m_archive_size - file_ofs) as usize);
            p_buf[..n].copy_from_slice(&mem[file_ofs as usize..file_ofs as usize + n]);
            return n;
        }
    }
    0
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_init_mem(
    p_zip: &mut ZipArchive,
    p_mem: &[u8],
    size: usize,
    flags: u32,
) -> bool {
    if p_mem.is_empty() {
        return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    if size < MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE {
        return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::NotAnArchive);
    }

    if !p_zip.reader_init_internal(flags) {
        return false;
    }

    p_zip.m_zip_type = ZipType::Memory;
    p_zip.m_archive_size = size as u64;

    if let Some(state) = &mut p_zip.m_p_state {
        state.p_mem = Some(p_mem.to_vec());
        state.mem_size = size;
    }

    if !mz_zip_reader_read_central_dir(p_zip, flags) {
        mz_zip_reader_end_internal(p_zip, false);
        return false;
    }

    true
}

fn mz_zip_file_read_func(p_opaque: &ZipArchive, file_ofs: u64, p_buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if let Some(state) = &p_opaque.m_p_state {
        if let Some(file) = &mut state.p_file {
            let absolute_ofs = file_ofs + state.file_archive_start_ofs;
            if file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(absolute_ofs)).is_ok() {
                return file.read(p_buf).unwrap_or(0);
            }
        }
    }
    0
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_init_file(p_zip: &mut ZipArchive, p_filename: &str, flags: u32) -> bool {
    mz_zip_reader_init_file_v2(p_zip, p_filename, flags, 0, 0)
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_init_file_v2(
    p_zip: &mut ZipArchive,
    p_filename: &str,
    flags: u32,
    file_start_ofs: u64,
    archive_size: u64,
) -> bool {
    if p_filename.is_empty() || (archive_size > 0 && archive_size < MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64) {
        return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    let file = match File::open(p_filename) {
        Ok(f) => f,
        Err(_) => return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::FileOpenFailed),
    };

    let file_size = if archive_size == 0 {
        match file.metadata() {
            Ok(meta) => meta.len(),
            Err(_) => {
                return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::FileSeekFailed);
            }
        }
    } else {
        archive_size
    };

    if file_size < MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 {
        return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::NotAnArchive);
    }

    if !p_zip.reader_init_internal(flags) {
        return false;
    }

    p_zip.m_zip_type = ZipType::File;
    p_zip.m_archive_size = file_size;

    if let Some(state) = &mut p_zip.m_p_state {
        state.p_file = Some(file);
        state.file_archive_start_ofs = file_start_ofs;
    }

    if !mz_zip_reader_read_central_dir(p_zip, flags) {
        mz_zip_reader_end_internal(p_zip, false);
        return false;
    }

    true
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_init_cfile(
    p_zip: &mut ZipArchive,
    p_file: File,
    archive_size: u64,
    flags: u32,
) -> bool {
    let cur_file_ofs = match p_file.seek(SeekFrom::Current(0)) {
        Ok(pos) => pos,
        Err(_) => return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::FileSeekFailed),
    };

    let actual_archive_size = if archive_size == 0 {
        match p_file.metadata() {
            Ok(meta) => meta.len() - cur_file_ofs,
            Err(_) => return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::FileSeekFailed),
        }
    } else {
        archive_size
    };

    if actual_archive_size < MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 {
        return p_zip.set_error(ZipError::NotAnArchive);
    }

    if !p_zip.reader_init_internal(flags) {
        return false;
    }

    p_zip.m_zip_type = ZipType::CFile;
    p_zip.m_archive_size = actual_archive_size;

    if let Some(state) = &mut p_zip.m_p_state {
        state.p_file = Some(p_file);
        state.file_archive_start_ofs = cur_file_ofs;
    }

    if !mz_zip_reader_read_central_dir(p_zip, flags) {
        mz_zip_reader_end_internal(p_zip, false);
        return false;
    }

    true
}

fn mz_zip_get_cdh(p_zip: &ZipArchive, file_index: u32) -> Option<&[u8]> {
    let state = p_zip.m_p_state.as_ref()?;
    if file_index >= p_zip.m_total_files {
        return None;
    }

    let offset = state.central_dir_offsets.data.get(file_index as usize)?;
    let start = *offset as usize;
    let end = start + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE;

    if end <= state.central_dir.data.len() {
        Some(&state.central_dir.data[start..end])
    } else {
        None
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_is_file_encrypted(p_zip: &mut ZipArchive, file_index: u32) -> bool {
    let cdh = match mz_zip_get_cdh(p_zip, file_index) {
        Some(c) => c,
        None => {
            p_zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
            return false;
        }
    };

    let bit_flag = read_le16(&cdh[MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS..]);
    (bit_flag & (MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_IS_ENCRYPTED
        | MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_USES_STRONG_ENCRYPTION))
        != 0
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_is_file_supported(p_zip: &mut ZipArchive, file_index: u32) -> bool {
    let cdh = match mz_zip_get_cdh(p_zip, file_index) {
        Some(c) => c,
        None => {
            p_zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
            return false;
        }
    };

    let method = read_le16(&cdh[MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS..]);
    let bit_flag = read_le16(&cdh[MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS..]);

    if method != 0 && method != 8 {
        // 8 = MZ_DEFLATED
        p_zip.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedMethod);
        return false;
    }

    if (bit_flag
        & (MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_IS_ENCRYPTED
            | MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_USES_STRONG_ENCRYPTION))
        != 0
    {
        p_zip.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedEncryption);
        return false;
    }

    if (bit_flag & MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_COMPRESSED_PATCH_FLAG) != 0 {
        p_zip.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedFeature);
        return false;
    }

    true
}

#[derive(Debug)]

impl<T> ZipArray<T> {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { data: Vec::new() }
    }

    pub fn as_slice(&self) -> &[T] {
        &self.data
    }

    pub fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.data.len()
    }

    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.data.is_empty()
    }

    pub fn get(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&T> {
        self.data.get(index)
    }
}


#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct ZipArchiveFileStat {
    pub file_index: u32,
    pub central_dir_ofs: u32,
    pub version_made_by: u16,
    pub version_needed: u16,
    pub bit_flag: u16,
    pub method: u16,
    pub crc32: u32,
    pub comp_size: u64,
    pub uncomp_size: u64,
    pub internal_attr: u16,
    pub external_attr: u32,
    pub local_header_ofs: u64,
    pub filename: Vec<u8>,
    pub comment: Vec<u8>,
    pub is_directory: bool,
    pub is_encrypted: bool,
    pub is_supported: bool,
}


impl ZipArchive {
    fn set_error(&mut self, error: ZipError) -> bool {
        self.error = Some(error);
        false
    }

    fn get_error(&self) -> Option<ZipError> {
        self.error
    }

    fn clear_error(&mut self) {
        self.error = None;
    }
}

// Helper functions
fn mz_tolower(c: u8) -> u8 {
    if c >= b'A' && c <= b'Z' {
        c - b'A' + b'a'
    } else {
        c
    }
}

fn mz_min<T: Ord>(a: T, b: T) -> T {
    if a < b { a } else { b }
}

fn mz_max<T: Ord>(a: T, b: T) -> T {
    if a > b { a } else { b }
}

fn mz_read_le16(p: &[u8]) -> u16 {
    u16::from_le_bytes([p[0], p[1]])
}

fn mz_read_le32(p: &[u8]) -> u32 {
    u32::from_le_bytes([p[0], p[1], p[2], p[3]])
}

fn mz_read_le64(p: &[u8]) -> u64 {
    u64::from_le_bytes([p[0], p[1], p[2], p[3], p[4], p[5], p[6], p[7]])
}

fn mz_crc32(init: u32, buf: &[u8]) -> u32 {
    let mut crc = init;
    for &byte in buf {
        crc = crc.wrapping_add(byte as u32).wrapping_mul(0x1000193);
    }
    crc
}

const MZ_CRC32_INIT: u32 = 0;

const TINFL_FLAG_USING_NON_WRAPPING_OUTPUT_BUF: u32 = 0x2000;
const TINFL_FLAG_HAS_MORE_INPUT: u32 = 0x8;

// Main functions
pub fn mz_zip_reader_is_file_a_directory(zip: &mut ZipArchive, file_index: u32) -> bool {
    // Clear any previous error
    zip.clear_error();

    // Get central directory header
    let state = match &zip.state {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter),
    };

    if file_index as usize >= state.central_dir_offsets.len() {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    let offset = state.central_dir_offsets.get(file_index as usize)
        .copied()
        .unwrap_or(0) as usize;
    
    if offset + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE > state.central_dir.len() {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    let p = &state.central_dir.as_slice()[offset..];

    // Check filename
    let filename_len = mz_read_le16(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
    if filename_len > 0 {
        let filename_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_len - 1;
        if filename_start < p.len() && p[filename_start] == b'/' {
            return true;
        }
    }

    // Check DOS directory attribute
    let external_attr = mz_read_le32(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTERNAL_ATTR_OFS..]);
    if (external_attr & MZ_ZIP_DOS_DIR_ATTRIBUTE_BITFLAG) != 0 {
        return true;
    }

    false
}

fn mz_zip_file_stat_internal(
    zip: &mut ZipArchive,
    file_index: u32,
    p_central_dir_header: &[u8],
    p_stat: &mut ZipArchiveFileStat,
    p_found_zip64_extra_data: Option<&mut bool>,
) -> bool {
    // Clear any previous error
    zip.clear_error();

    if p_central_dir_header.is_empty() {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    let p = p_central_dir_header;

    if let Some(found) = p_found_zip64_extra_data {
        *found = false;
    }

    // Extract basic fields
    p_stat.file_index = file_index;
    
    if let Some(state) = &zip.state {
        if file_index as usize < state.central_dir_offsets.len() {
            p_stat.central_dir_ofs = *state.central_dir_offsets.get(file_index as usize).unwrap();
        }
    }

    p_stat.version_made_by = mz_read_le16(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_VERSION_MADE_BY_OFS..]);
    p_stat.version_needed = mz_read_le16(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_VERSION_NEEDED_OFS..]);
    p_stat.bit_flag = mz_read_le16(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS..]);
    p_stat.method = mz_read_le16(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS..]);
    p_stat.crc32 = mz_read_le32(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_CRC32_OFS..]);
    p_stat.comp_size = mz_read_le32(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..]) as u64;
    p_stat.uncomp_size = mz_read_le32(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..]) as u64;
    p_stat.internal_attr = mz_read_le16(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_INTERNAL_ATTR_OFS..]);
    p_stat.external_attr = mz_read_le32(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTERNAL_ATTR_OFS..]);
    p_stat.local_header_ofs = mz_read_le32(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS..]) as u64;

    // Copy filename
    let filename_len = mz_read_le16(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
    let filename_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE;
    if filename_start + filename_len <= p.len() {
        p_stat.filename = p[filename_start..filename_start + filename_len].to_vec();
    }

    // Copy comment
    let comment_len = mz_read_le16(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
    let extra_len = mz_read_le16(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
    let comment_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_len + extra_len;
    if comment_start + comment_len <= p.len() {
        p_stat.comment = p[comment_start..comment_start + comment_len].to_vec();
    }

    // Set convenience flags
    p_stat.is_directory = mz_zip_reader_is_file_a_directory(zip, file_index);
    // Note: mz_zip_reader_is_file_encrypted and mz_zip_reader_is_file_supported
    // would need to be implemented separately
    p_stat.is_encrypted = (p_stat.bit_flag & MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_IS_ENCRYPTED) != 0;
    p_stat.is_supported = p_stat.method == 0 || p_stat.method == MZ_DEFLATED;

    // Check for ZIP64 extended information
    if mz_max(mz_max(p_stat.comp_size, p_stat.uncomp_size), p_stat.local_header_ofs) == MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 {
        let extra_size_remaining = mz_read_le16(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
        
        if extra_size_remaining > 0 {
            let mut p_extra_data = &p[MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_len..];
            let mut remaining = extra_size_remaining;
            
            while remaining >= 4 {
                let field_id = mz_read_le16(p_extra_data);
                let field_data_size = mz_read_le16(&p_extra_data[2..]) as usize;
                
                if field_data_size + 4 > remaining {
                    return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                }
                
                if field_id == MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID {
                    if let Some(found) = p_found_zip64_extra_data {
                        *found = true;
                    }
                    
                    let mut p_field_data = &p_extra_data[4..];
                    let mut field_data_remaining = field_data_size;
                    
                    if p_stat.uncomp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 {
                        if field_data_remaining < 8 {
                            return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                        }
                        p_stat.uncomp_size = mz_read_le64(p_field_data);
                        p_field_data = &p_field_data[8..];
                        field_data_remaining -= 8;
                    }
                    
                    if p_stat.comp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 {
                        if field_data_remaining < 8 {
                            return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                        }
                        p_stat.comp_size = mz_read_le64(p_field_data);
                        p_field_data = &p_field_data[8..];
                        field_data_remaining -= 8;
                    }
                    
                    if p_stat.local_header_ofs == MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 {
                        if field_data_remaining < 8 {
                            return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                        }
                        p_stat.local_header_ofs = mz_read_le64(p_field_data);
                    }
                    
                    break;
                }
                
                let advance = 4 + field_data_size;
                if advance > p_extra_data.len() {
                    break;
                }
                p_extra_data = &p_extra_data[advance..];
                remaining = remaining.saturating_sub(advance);
            }
        }
    }

    true
}

fn mz_zip_string_equal(p_a: &[u8], p_b: &[u8], len: usize, flags: u32) -> bool {
    if flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_CASE_SENSITIVE != 0 {
        p_a[..len] == p_b[..len]
    } else {
        p_a[..len].iter().zip(p_b[..len].iter()).all(|(&a, &b)| {
            mz_tolower(a) == mz_tolower(b)
        })
    }
}

fn mz_zip_filename_compare(
    central_dir_array: &ZipArray<u8>,
    central_dir_offsets: &ZipArray<u32>,
    l_index: u32,
    p_r: &[u8],
    r_len: usize,
) -> std::cmp::Ordering {
    let offset = match central_dir_offsets.get(l_index as usize) {
        Some(&off) => off as usize,
        None => return Ordering::Greater, // Invalid index
    };

    if offset + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE > central_dir_array.len() {
        return Ordering::Greater;
    }

    let p_l = &central_dir_array.as_slice()[offset..];
    let l_len = mz_read_le16(&p_l[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
    
    let filename_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE;
    let compare_len = mz_min(l_len, r_len);
    
    for i in 0..compare_len {
        let l_byte = if filename_start + i < p_l.len() {
            mz_tolower(p_l[filename_start + i])
        } else {
            0
        };
        
        let r_byte = if i < p_r.len() {
            mz_tolower(p_r[i])
        } else {
            0
        };
        
        match l_byte.cmp(&r_byte) {
            Ordering::Equal => continue,
            non_eq => return non_eq,
        }
    }
    
    l_len.cmp(&r_len)
}

fn mz_zip_locate_file_binary_search(
    zip: &mut ZipArchive,
    filename: &str,
    p_index: Option<&mut u32>,
) -> bool {
    // Clear any previous error
    zip.clear_error();

    let state = match &zip.state {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter),
    };

    if let Some(idx) = p_index {
        *idx = 0;
    }

    let size = zip.total_files;
    if size == 0 {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::FileNotFound);
    }

    let filename_bytes = filename.as_bytes();
    let mut l: i64 = 0;
    let mut h: i64 = size as i64 - 1;

    while l <= h {
        let m = l + ((h - l) >> 1);
        let file_index = state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.get(m as usize)
            .copied()
            .unwrap_or(0);

        let comp = mz_zip_filename_compare(
            &state.central_dir,
            &state.central_dir_offsets,
            file_index,
            filename_bytes,
            filename_bytes.len(),
        );

        match comp.cmp(&0) {
            Ordering::Equal => {
                if let Some(idx) = p_index {
                    *idx = file_index;
                }
                return true;
            }
            Ordering::Less => l = m + 1,
            Ordering::Greater => h = m - 1,
        }
    }

    zip.set_error(ZipError::FileNotFound)
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_locate_file(
    zip: &mut ZipArchive,
    name: &str,
    comment: Option<&str>,
    flags: u32,
) -> Result<u32, ZipError> {
    let mut index = 0;
    if mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(zip, name, comment, flags, Some(&mut index)) {
        Ok(index)
    } else {
        Err(zip.get_error().unwrap_or(ZipError::FileNotFound))
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(
    zip: &mut ZipArchive,
    name: &str,
    comment: Option<&str>,
    flags: u32,
    p_index: Option<&mut u32>,
) -> bool {
    // Clear any previous error
    zip.clear_error();

    if p_index.is_some() {
        if let Some(idx) = p_index {
            *idx = 0;
        }
    }

    let state = match &zip.state {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter),
    };

    // Try binary search if conditions allow
    if (state.init_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_DO_NOT_SORT_CENTRAL_DIRECTORY) == 0 &&
        zip.zip_mode == MZ_ZIP_MODE_READING &&
        (flags & (MZ_ZIP_FLAG_IGNORE_PATH | MZ_ZIP_FLAG_CASE_SENSITIVE)) == 0 &&
        comment.is_none() &&
        !state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.is_empty()
    {
        return mz_zip_locate_file_binary_search(zip, name, p_index);
    }

    // Otherwise scan linearly
    let name_bytes = name.as_bytes();
    let name_len = name_bytes.len();
    if name_len > u16::MAX as usize {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    let comment_bytes = comment.map(|s| s.as_bytes());
    let comment_len = comment_bytes.map(|b| b.len()).unwrap_or(0);
    if comment_len > u16::MAX as usize {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    for file_index in 0..zip.total_files {
        let offset = match state.central_dir_offsets.get(file_index as usize) {
            Some(&off) => off as usize,
            None => continue,
        };

        if offset + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE > state.central_dir.len() {
            continue;
        }

        let p_header = &state.central_dir.as_slice()[offset..];
        let filename_len = mz_read_le16(&p_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
        
        if filename_len < name_len {
            continue;
        }

        // Check comment if provided
        if let Some(comment_bytes) = comment_bytes {
            let file_extra_len = mz_read_le16(&p_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
            let file_comment_len = mz_read_le16(&p_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
            
            if file_comment_len != comment_len {
                continue;
            }
            
            let comment_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_len + file_extra_len;
            if comment_start + file_comment_len > p_header.len() {
                continue;
            }
            
            let file_comment = &p_header[comment_start..comment_start + file_comment_len];
            if !mz_zip_string_equal(comment_bytes, file_comment, comment_len, flags) {
                continue;
            }
        }

        let mut p_filename = &p_header[MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE..];
        let mut actual_filename_len = filename_len;

        // Handle ignore path flag
        if flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_IGNORE_PATH != 0 && filename_len > 0 {
            let mut ofs = filename_len - 1;
            while ofs > 0 {
                let ch = p_filename[ofs];
                if ch == b'/' || ch == b'\\' || ch == b':' {
                    break;
                }
                ofs -= 1;
            }
            if p_filename[ofs] == b'/' || p_filename[ofs] == b'\\' || p_filename[ofs] == b':' {
                p_filename = &p_filename[ofs + 1..];
                actual_filename_len -= ofs + 1;
            }
        }

        if actual_filename_len == name_len &&
            mz_zip_string_equal(name_bytes, &p_filename[..actual_filename_len], name_len, flags)
        {
            if let Some(idx) = p_index {
                *idx = file_index;
            }
            return true;
        }
    }

    zip.set_error(ZipError::FileNotFound)
}

fn mz_zip_reader_extract_to_mem_no_alloc1(
    zip: &mut ZipArchive,
    file_index: u32,
    buf: &mut [u8],
    buf_size: usize,
    flags: u32,
    user_read_buf: Option<&mut [u8]>,
    user_read_buf_size: usize,
    st: Option<&ZipArchiveFileStat>,
) -> bool {
    // Clear any previous error
    zip.clear_error();

    if buf_size > 0 && buf.is_empty() {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    if user_read_buf_size > 0 && user_read_buf.is_none() {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    if zip.p_read.is_none() {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    // Get file stat
    let mut file_stat = if let Some(stat) = st {
        stat.clone()
    } else {
        // Note: mz_zip_reader_file_stat would need to be implemented
        return false;
    };

    // Directory or zero-length file
    if file_stat.is_directory || file_stat.comp_size == 0 {
        return true;
    }

    // Check for unsupported encryption
    if file_stat.bit_flag & (MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_IS_ENCRYPTED |
        MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_USES_STRONG_ENCRYPTION |
        MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_COMPRESSED_PATCH_FLAG) != 0
    {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedEncryption);
    }

    // Check supported methods
    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 &&
        file_stat.method != 0 &&
        file_stat.method != MZ_DEFLATED
    {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedMethod);
    }

    // Check buffer size
    let needed_size = if flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA != 0 {
        file_stat.comp_size as usize
    } else {
        file_stat.uncomp_size as usize
    };

    if buf_size < needed_size {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::BufTooSmall);
    }

    // Read local header
    let mut local_header = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
    let read_fn = zip.p_read.unwrap();
    let read_result = read_fn(
        zip.p_io_opaque,
        file_stat.local_header_ofs,
        local_header.as_mut_ptr(),
        MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE,
    );

    if read_result != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
    }

    if mz_read_le32(&local_header) != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }

    // Calculate start of file data
    let filename_len = mz_read_le16(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..]) as u64;
    let extra_len = mz_read_le16(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..]) as u64;
    let cur_file_ofs = file_stat.local_header_ofs +
        MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 +
        filename_len +
        extra_len;

    if cur_file_ofs + file_stat.comp_size > zip.archive_size {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }

    // Handle stored data or compressed data request
    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) != 0 || file_stat.method == 0 {
        let read_result = read_fn(
            zip.p_io_opaque,
            cur_file_ofs,
            buf.as_mut_ptr(),
            needed_size,
        );

        if read_result != needed_size {
            return zip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
        }

        // Verify CRC if not compressed data
        if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 {
            let crc = mz_crc32(MZ_CRC32_INIT, &buf[..file_stat.uncomp_size as usize]);
            if crc != file_stat.crc32 {
                return zip.set_error(ZipError::CrcCheckFailed);
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    // Handle decompression
    let mut inflator = TinflDecompressor::new();
    tinfl_init(&mut inflator);

    let state = zip.state.as_ref().unwrap();
    let (p_read_buf, read_buf_size, mut read_buf_avail, mut comp_remaining) = if state.p_mem.is_some() {
        // Read from memory archive
        let mem_start = cur_file_ofs as usize;
        let slice = &state.p_mem.as_ref().unwrap()[mem_start..];
        let avail = mz_min(slice.len(), file_stat.comp_size as usize);
        (slice.as_ptr(), avail, avail, 0)
    } else if let Some(user_buf) = user_read_buf {
        // Use user buffer
        if user_read_buf_size == 0 {
            return false;
        }
        (user_buf.as_ptr(), user_read_buf_size, 0, file_stat.comp_size as usize)
    } else {
        // Allocate buffer
        let read_buf_size = mz_min(file_stat.comp_size as usize, 65536); // MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE
        if read_buf_size == 0 {
            return zip.set_error(ZipError::InternalError);
        }
        
        let alloc_fn = match zip.p_alloc {
            Some(f) => f,
            None => return zip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed),
        };
        
        let p_buf = alloc_fn(zip.p_alloc_opaque, 1, read_buf_size);
        if p_buf.is_null() {
            return zip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
        }
        
        (p_buf as *mut u8, read_buf_size, 0, file_stat.comp_size as usize)
    };

    let mut out_buf_ofs = 0;
    let mut read_buf_ofs = 0;
    let mut cur_ofs = cur_file_ofs;
    let mut status = TinflStatus::Done;

    loop {
        let out_buf_size = (file_stat.uncomp_size as usize) - out_buf_ofs;
        
        // Read more data if needed
        if read_buf_avail == 0 && comp_remaining > 0 {
            let to_read = mz_min(read_buf_size, comp_remaining);
            if to_read > 0 {
                let read_result = read_fn(
                    zip.p_io_opaque,
                    cur_ofs,
                    unsafe { p_read_buf.add(read_buf_ofs) },
                    to_read,
                );
                
                if read_result != to_read {
                    status = TinflStatus::Failed;
                    zip.set_error(ZipError::DecompressionFailed);
                    break;
                }
                
                cur_ofs += to_read as u64;
                comp_remaining -= to_read;
                read_buf_avail = to_read;
                read_buf_ofs = 0;
            }
        }
        
        let in_buf_size = read_buf_avail;
        let (new_status, in_used, out_used) = inflator.decompress(
            unsafe { slice::from_raw_parts(p_read_buf.add(read_buf_ofs), in_buf_size) },
            &mut buf[out_buf_ofs..out_buf_ofs + out_buf_size],
            TINFL_FLAG_USING_NON_WRAPPING_OUTPUT_BUF |
                if comp_remaining > 0 { TINFL_FLAG_HAS_MORE_INPUT } else { 0 },
        );
        
        status = new_status;
        read_buf_avail -= in_used;
        read_buf_ofs += in_used;
        out_buf_ofs += out_used;
        
        if status != TinflStatus::NeedsMoreInput {
            break;
        }
    }

    // Cleanup
    if state.p_mem.is_none() && user_read_buf.is_none() {
        if let Some(free_fn) = zip.p_free {
            free_fn(zip.p_alloc_opaque, p_read_buf as *mut std::ffi::c_void);
        }
    }

    if status == TinflStatus::Done {
        if out_buf_ofs != file_stat.uncomp_size as usize {
            zip.set_error(ZipError::UnexpectedDecompressedSize);
            return false;
        }
        
        let crc = mz_crc32(MZ_CRC32_INIT, &buf[..file_stat.uncomp_size as usize]);
        if crc != file_stat.crc32 {
            zip.set_error(ZipError::CrcCheckFailed);
            return false;
        }
        
        true
    } else {
        false
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_to_mem_no_alloc(
    zip: &mut ZipArchive,
    file_index: u32,
    buf: &mut [u8],
    buf_size: usize,
    flags: u32,
    user_read_buf: Option<&mut [u8]>,
    user_read_buf_size: usize,
) -> bool {
    mz_zip_reader_extract_to_mem_no_alloc1(
        zip,
        file_index,
        buf,
        buf_size,
        flags,
        user_read_buf,
        user_read_buf_size,
        None,
    )
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_file_to_mem_no_alloc(
    zip: &mut ZipArchive,
    filename: &str,
    buf: &mut [u8],
    buf_size: usize,
    flags: u32,
    user_read_buf: Option<&mut [u8]>,
    user_read_buf_size: usize,
) -> bool {
    let mut file_index = 0;
    if !mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(zip, filename, None, flags, Some(&mut file_index)) {
        return false;
    }
    
    mz_zip_reader_extract_to_mem_no_alloc1(
        zip,
        file_index,
        buf,
        buf_size,
        flags,
        user_read_buf,
        user_read_buf_size,
        None,
    )
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_to_mem(
    zip: &mut ZipArchive,
    file_index: u32,
    buf: &mut [u8],
    buf_size: usize,
    flags: u32,
) -> bool {
    mz_zip_reader_extract_to_mem_no_alloc1(
        zip,
        file_index,
        buf,
        buf_size,
        flags,
        None,
        0,
        None,
    )
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_file_to_mem(
    zip: &mut ZipArchive,
    filename: &str,
    buf: &mut [u8],
    buf_size: usize,
    flags: u32,
) -> bool {
    mz_zip_reader_extract_file_to_mem_no_alloc(
        zip,
        filename,
        buf,
        buf_size,
        flags,
        None,
        0,
    )
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_to_heap(
    zip: &mut ZipArchive,
    file_index: u32,
    p_size: Option<&mut usize>,
    flags: u32,
) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
    if let Some(size) = p_size {
        *size = 0;
    }

    // Get file stat
    let file_stat = {
        // Note: mz_zip_reader_file_stat would need to be implemented
        return None;
    };

    let alloc_size = if flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA != 0 {
        file_stat.comp_size as usize
    } else {
        file_stat.uncomp_size as usize
    };

    if alloc_size > isize::MAX as usize {
        zip.set_error(ZipError::InternalError);
        return None;
    }

    let alloc_fn = match zip.p_alloc {
        Some(f) => f,
        None => {
            zip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
            return None;
        }
    };

    let p_buf = alloc_fn(zip.p_alloc_opaque, 1, alloc_size);
    if p_buf.is_null() {
        zip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
        return None;
    }

    let mut buf_vec = unsafe {
        Vec::from_raw_parts(p_buf as *mut u8, alloc_size, alloc_size)
    };

    if !mz_zip_reader_extract_to_mem_no_alloc1(
        zip,
        file_index,
        &mut buf_vec,
        alloc_size,
        flags,
        None,
        0,
        Some(&file_stat),
    ) {
        if let Some(free_fn) = zip.p_free {
            free_fn(zip.p_alloc_opaque, buf_vec.as_mut_ptr() as *mut std::ffi::c_void);
        }
        // Prevent double-free
        std::mem::forget(buf_vec);
        return None;
    }

    if let Some(size) = p_size {
        *size = alloc_size;
    }

    Some(buf_vec)
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_file_to_heap(
    zip: &mut ZipArchive,
    filename: &str,
    p_size: Option<&mut usize>,
    flags: u32,
) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut file_index = 0;
    if !mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(zip, filename, None, flags, Some(&mut file_index)) {
        if let Some(size) = p_size {
            *size = 0;
        }
        return None;
    }
    
    mz_zip_reader_extract_to_heap(zip, file_index, p_size, flags)
}

// --- Module: mz_p4 ---
// ZIP extraction callbacks and iterator implementation

// Constants needed for implementation
const MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE: usize = 64 * 1024;

// Type definitions
pub type MzFileWriteFunc = fn(opaque: &mut dyn std::any::Any, file_ofs: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> usize;


pub struct MzZipState {
    m_p_mem: Vec<u8>,
    m_mem_size: usize,
}



// Error type enum
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum MzZipError {
    InvalidParameter,
    FileNotFound,
    FileReadFailed,
    InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted,
    UnsupportedEncryption,
    UnsupportedMethod,
    WriteCallbackFailed,
    UnexpectedDecompressedSize,
    DecompressionFailed,
    UnsupportedFeature,
    FileOpenFailed,
    FileCloseFailed,
    AllocFailed,
}

impl Default for MzZipArchiveFileStat {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            m_file_index: 0,
            m_central_dir_ofs: 0,
            m_version_made_by: 0,
            m_version_needed: 0,
            m_bit_flag: 0,
            m_method: 0,
            m_time: 0,
            m_crc32: 0,
            m_comp_size: 0,
            m_uncomp_size: 0,
            m_internal_attr: 0,
            m_external_attr: 0,
            m_local_header_ofs: 0,
            m_filename: String::new(),
            m_comment: String::new(),
            m_comment_size: 0,
            m_is_directory: false,
            m_is_encrypted: false,
            m_is_supported: false,
        }
    }
}

    fn read_data(&self, file_ofs: u64, buf: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, MzZipError> {

        Ok(0)
    }
}

    // Placeholder implementation
    Ok(true)
}

    // Placeholder implementation
    Ok(true)
}

fn mz_crc32(crc: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> u64 {
    // Placeholder implementation
    crc
}

/// Extract a file from ZIP archive to a callback function
pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_to_callback(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    file_index: u32,
    p_callback: MzFileWriteFunc,
    p_opaque: &mut dyn std::any::Any,
    flags: u32,
) -> Result<bool, MzZipError> {
    
    if p_zip.m_p_state.is_none() || p_callback as usize == 0 || p_zip.m_p_read.is_none() {
        return Err(InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    let mut file_stat = MzZipArchiveFileStat::default();
    if !mz_zip_reader_file_stat(p_zip, file_index, &mut file_stat)? {
        return Ok(false);
    }
    
    // A directory or zero length file
    if file_stat.m_is_directory || file_stat.m_comp_size == 0 {
        return Ok(true);
    }
    
    // Encryption and patch files are not supported
    let unsupported_flags = MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_IS_ENCRYPTED |
                          MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_USES_STRONG_ENCRYPTION |
                          MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_COMPRESSED_PATCH_FLAG;
    if file_stat.m_bit_flag & unsupported_flags != 0 {
        return Err(UnsupportedEncryption);
    }
    
    // This function only supports decompressing stored and deflate
    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 &&
       file_stat.m_method != 0 && file_stat.m_method != MZ_DEFLATED {
        return Err(UnsupportedMethod);
    }
    
    // Read and validate local directory entry
    let mut local_header = [0u8; 30];
    let cur_file_ofs = file_stat.m_local_header_ofs;
    
    let bytes_read = p_zip.read_data(cur_file_ofs, &mut local_header)?;
    if bytes_read != 30 {
        return Err(FileReadFailed);
    }
    
    // Check signature
    if u32::from_le_bytes(local_header[0..4].try_into().unwrap()) != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
        return Err(InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }
    
    // Calculate data offset
    let filename_len = u16::from_le_bytes(local_header[26..28].try_into().unwrap()) as u64;
    let extra_len = u16::from_le_bytes(local_header[28..30].try_into().unwrap()) as u64;
    let data_ofs = cur_file_ofs + 30u64 + filename_len + extra_len;
    
    if data_ofs + file_stat.m_comp_size > p_zip.m_archive_size {
        return Err(InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }
    
    // Decompress or copy the file
    let mut file_crc32 = MZ_CRC32_INIT;
    let mut out_buf_ofs: u64 = 0;
    
    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) != 0 || file_stat.m_method == 0 {
        // Stored or compressed data requested
        extract_stored(p_zip, data_ofs, file_stat.m_comp_size, 
                      p_callback, p_opaque, &mut file_crc32, &mut out_buf_ofs, flags)?
    } else {
        // Deflate decompression required
        extract_deflated(p_zip, data_ofs, file_stat.m_comp_size, file_stat.m_uncomp_size,
                        p_callback, p_opaque, &mut file_crc32, &mut out_buf_ofs)?
    }
    
    // Verify decompression
    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 {
        if out_buf_ofs != file_stat.m_uncomp_size {
            return Err(UnexpectedDecompressedSize);
        }
        
        if cfg!(not(feature = "disable_crc32_checks")) {
            if file_crc32 != file_stat.m_crc32 {
                return Err(DecompressionFailed);
            }
        }
    }
    
    Ok(true)
}

/// Extract stored (uncompressed) data
fn extract_stored(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    data_ofs: u64,
    comp_size: u64,
    p_callback: MzFileWriteFunc,
    p_opaque: &mut dyn std::any::Any,
    file_crc32: &mut u32,
    out_buf_ofs: &mut u64,
    flags: u32,
) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
    let buffer_size = comp_size.min(MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE as u64) as usize;
    let mut buffer = vec![0u8; buffer_size];
    let mut remaining = comp_size;
    let mut cur_ofs = data_ofs;
    
    while remaining > 0 {
        let to_read = remaining.min(buffer_size as u64) as usize;
        let read = p_zip.read_data(cur_ofs, &mut buffer[..to_read])?;
        if read != to_read {
            return Err(MzZipError::FileReadFailed);
        }
        
        // Compute CRC if not returning compressed data only
        if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 {
            *file_crc32 = mz_crc32(*file_crc32 as u64, &buffer[..read]) as u32;
        }
        
        // Write to callback
        let written = p_callback(p_opaque, *out_buf_ofs, &buffer[..read]);
        if written != read {
            return Err(MzZipError::WriteCallbackFailed);
        }
        
        cur_ofs += read as u64;
        *out_buf_ofs += read as u64;
        remaining -= read as u64;
    }
    
    Ok(())
}

/// Extract deflated data
fn extract_deflated(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    data_ofs: u64,
    comp_size: u64,
    uncomp_size: u64,
    p_callback: MzFileWriteFunc,
    p_opaque: &mut dyn std::any::Any,
    file_crc32: &mut u32,
    out_buf_ofs: &mut u64,
) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
    // Note: tinfl_decompressor implementation would need to be provided
    // This is a simplified placeholder - actual implementation requires tinfl
    Err(MzZipError::UnsupportedMethod)
}

/// Extract a file by name to a callback function
pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_file_to_callback(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    filename: &str,
    p_callback: MzFileWriteFunc,
    p_opaque: &mut dyn std::any::Any,
    flags: u32,
) -> Result<bool, MzZipError> {
    let mut file_index = 0u32;
    if !mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(p_zip, filename, None, flags, &mut file_index)? {
        return Ok(false);
    }
    
    mz_zip_reader_extract_to_callback(p_zip, file_index, p_callback, p_opaque, flags)
}

/// Create new extraction iterator state
pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_iter_new(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    file_index: u32,
    flags: u32,
) -> Result<Box<MzZipReaderExtractIterState>, MzZipError> {
    
    if p_zip.m_p_state.is_none() {
        return Err(InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    let mut state = Box::new(MzZipReaderExtractIterState {
        p_zip: Box::new(MzZipArchive {
            m_p_state: p_zip.m_p_state.clone(),
            m_p_read: p_zip.m_p_read,
            m_archive_size: p_zip.m_archive_size,
            m_file_archive_start_ofs: p_zip.m_file_archive_start_ofs,
        }),
        file_stat: MzZipArchiveFileStat::default(),
        read_buf: Vec::new(),
        read_buf_size: 0,
        read_buf_ofs: 0,
        read_buf_avail: 0,
        comp_remaining: 0,
        out_buf_ofs: 0,
        cur_file_ofs: 0,
        status: 0,
    });
    
    // Fetch file details
    if !mz_zip_reader_file_stat(p_zip, file_index, &mut state.file_stat)? {
        return Err(InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    // Encryption and patch files are not supported
    let unsupported_flags = MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_IS_ENCRYPTED |
                          MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_USES_STRONG_ENCRYPTION |
                          MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_COMPRESSED_PATCH_FLAG;
    if state.file_stat.m_bit_flag & unsupported_flags != 0 {
        return Err(UnsupportedEncryption);
    }
    
    // Only support stored and deflate
    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 &&
       state.file_stat.m_method != 0 && state.file_stat.m_method != MZ_DEFLATED {
        return Err(UnsupportedMethod);
    }
    
    // Read and parse local directory entry
    let mut local_header = [0u8; 30];
    state.cur_file_ofs = state.file_stat.m_local_header_ofs;
    
    let bytes_read = p_zip.read_data(state.cur_file_ofs, &mut local_header)?;
    if bytes_read != 30 {
        return Err(FileReadFailed);
    }
    
    // Check signature
    if u32::from_le_bytes(local_header[0..4].try_into().unwrap()) != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
        return Err(InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }
    
    // Calculate data offset
    let filename_len = u16::from_le_bytes(local_header[26..28].try_into().unwrap()) as u64;
    let extra_len = u16::from_le_bytes(local_header[28..30].try_into().unwrap()) as u64;
    state.cur_file_ofs += 30u64 + filename_len + extra_len;
    
    if state.cur_file_ofs + state.file_stat.m_comp_size > p_zip.m_archive_size {
        return Err(InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }
    
    state.comp_remaining = state.file_stat.m_comp_size;
    state.status = 0; // TINFL_STATUS_DONE equivalent
    
    // Setup read buffer
    if state.file_stat.m_method == 0 || (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) != 0 {
        // Stored or compressed data - we'll read directly
        state.read_buf_size = 0;
    } else {
        // Deflate decompression - allocate buffer
        state.read_buf_size = state.file_stat.m_comp_size.min(MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE as u64) as usize;
        state.read_buf = vec![0u8; state.read_buf_size];
    }
    
    Ok(state)
}

/// Create extraction iterator by filename
pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_file_iter_new(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    filename: &str,
    flags: u32,
) -> Result<Box<MzZipReaderExtractIterState>, MzZipError> {
    let mut file_index = 0u32;
    if !mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(p_zip, filename, None, flags, &mut file_index)? {
        return Err(MzZipError::FileNotFound);
    }
    
    mz_zip_reader_extract_iter_new(p_zip, file_index, flags)
}

/// Read data from extraction iterator
pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_iter_read(
    state: &mut MzZipReaderExtractIterState,
    buf: &mut [u8],
    buf_size: usize,
) -> Result<usize, MzZipError> {
    // Note: This is a simplified version - actual implementation requires
    // tinfl decompression and proper state management
    if state.comp_remaining == 0 {
        return Ok(0);
    }
    
    let to_read = buf_size.min(state.comp_remaining as usize);
    let bytes_read = state.p_zip.read_data(state.cur_file_ofs, &mut buf[..to_read])?;
    
    state.cur_file_ofs += bytes_read as u64;
    state.comp_remaining -= bytes_read as u64;
    
    Ok(bytes_read)
}

// R12: truncated function stubbed at module boundary
pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_iter_free() -> bool { false }

// Error type for ZIP operations
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]

impl From<io::Error> for ZipError {
    fn from(_: io::Error) -> Self {
        ZipError::FileReadFailed
    }
}

// Main ZIP archive structure (simplified for this translation)


#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]

// ZIP array structure (replaces mz_zip_array)

impl<T> ZipArray<T> {
    fn new(element_size: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            m_p: Vec::new(),
            m_size: 0,
            m_capacity: 0,
            m_element_size: element_size,
        }
    }

    fn init(&mut self, element_size: usize) {
        self.m_p.clear();
        self.m_size = 0;
        self.m_capacity = 0;
        self.m_element_size = element_size;
    }

    fn clear(&mut self) {
        self.m_p.clear();
        self.m_size = 0;
        self.m_capacity = 0;
    }

    fn resize(&mut self, new_size: usize, zero_new: bool) -> bool {
        if new_size > self.m_capacity {
            let new_capacity = new_size.next_power_of_two();
            self.m_p.resize(new_capacity, T::default());
            self.m_capacity = new_capacity;
        }
        if zero_new && new_size > self.m_size {
            for i in self.m_size..new_size {
                self.m_p[i] = T::default();
            }
        }
        self.m_size = new_size;
        true
    }
}

impl<T: Default> Default for ZipArray<T> {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new(std::mem::size_of::<T>())
    }
}

// Function pointer types
type AllocFunc = fn(opaque: *mut (), items: usize, size: usize) -> *mut ();
type FreeFunc = fn(opaque: *mut (), ptr: *mut ());
type ReallocFunc = fn(opaque: *mut (), ptr: *mut (), new_size: usize) -> *mut ();
type ReadFunc = fn(opaque: *mut (), offset: u64, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize;
type WriteFunc = fn(opaque: *mut (), offset: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> usize;
