#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Debug)]
struct mz_zip_archive {
    m_pState: *mut mz_zip_internal_state,
    m_pAlloc: fn(opaque: *mut c_void, items: usize, size: usize) -> *mut c_void,
    m_pFree: fn(opaque: *mut c_void, address: *mut c_void),
    m_pRealloc: fn(opaque: *mut c_void, address: *mut c_void, new_size: usize) -> *mut c_void,
    m_pAlloc_opaque: *mut c_void,
    m_pRead: fn(p_zip: *mut mz_zip_archive, file_ofs: u64, p_buf: *mut c_void, n: usize) -> usize,
    m_pWrite: fn(p_zip: *mut mz_zip_archive, file_ofs: u64, p_buf: *const c_void, n: usize) -> usize,
    m_pNeutral_mem: *mut c_void,
    m_pIO_opaque: *mut c_void,
    m_pProgress: Option<fn(p_zip: *mut mz_zip_archive, percentage: u64) -> bool>,
    m_progress_opaque: *mut c_void,
    m_zip_mode: i32,
    m_file_offset_alignment: u32,
    m_total_files: u32,
    m_last_error: i32,
    m_archive_size: u64,
    m_central_directory_file_ofs: u64,
}

impl mz_zip_archive {
    fn default_alloc(_opaque: *mut c_void, items: usize, size: usize) -> *mut c_void {
        let total_size = items * size;
        if total_size == 0 {
            return ptr::null_mut();
        }
        unsafe {
            let layout = std::alloc::Layout::from_size_align(total_size, 1).unwrap();
            std::alloc::alloc(layout) as *mut c_void
        }
    }

    fn default_free(_opaque: *mut c_void, address: *mut c_void) {
        if !address.is_null() {
            unsafe {
                // Note: We don't know the size here, which is a limitation
                // In the real miniz code, they track sizes separately
                let layout = std::alloc::Layout::from_size_align(1, 1).unwrap();
                std::alloc::dealloc(address as *mut u8, layout);
            }
        }
    }

    fn default_realloc(_opaque: *mut c_void, address: *mut c_void, new_size: usize) -> *mut c_void {
        if address.is_null() {
            return Self::default_alloc(_opaque, 1, new_size);
        }
        
        if new_size == 0 {
            Self::default_free(_opaque, address);
            return ptr::null_mut();
        }
        
        unsafe {
            // Note: realloc without knowing old size is problematic
            // This is a simplified version
            let new_ptr = Self::default_alloc(_opaque, 1, new_size);
            if !new_ptr.is_null() && !address.is_null() {
                // Can't copy without knowing old size
                // This is why miniz tracks sizes in mz_zip_array
            }
            Self::default_free(_opaque, address);
            new_ptr
        }
    }

    fn new() -> Self {
        let state = Box::new(mz_zip_internal_state::new());
        let state_ptr = Box::into_raw(state);
        
        Self {
            m_pState: state_ptr,
            m_pAlloc: Self::default_alloc,
            m_pFree: Self::default_free,
            m_pRealloc: Self::default_realloc,
            m_pAlloc_opaque: ptr::null_mut(),
            m_pRead: Self::default_read,
            m_pWrite: Self::default_write,
            m_pNeutral_mem: ptr::null_mut(),
            m_pIO_opaque: ptr::null_mut(),
            m_pProgress: None,
            m_progress_opaque: ptr::null_mut(),
            m_zip_mode: 0, // MZ_ZIP_MODE_INVALID
            m_file_offset_alignment: 0,
            m_total_files: 0,
            m_last_error: 0, // MZ_ZIP_NO_ERROR
            m_archive_size: 0,
            m_central_directory_file_ofs: 0,
        }
    }

    unsafe fn default_read(
        p_zip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
        file_ofs: u64,
        p_buf: *mut c_void,
        n: usize,
    ) -> usize {
        let zip = &mut *p_zip;
        let state = &mut *zip.m_pState;
        
        if let Some(ref mut file) = state.m_pFile {
            if let Ok(_) = file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(file_ofs)) {
                let slice = std::slice::from_raw_parts_mut(p_buf as *mut u8, n);
                return file.read(slice).unwrap_or(0);
            }
        } else if !state.m_pMem.is_null() {
            let start = state.m_file_archive_start_ofs + file_ofs;
            if start + n as u64 <= state.m_mem_size as u64 {
                let src = state.m_pMem.add(start as usize);
                let dst = p_buf as *mut u8;
                ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(src, dst, n);
                return n;
            }
        }
        0
    }

    unsafe fn default_write(
        p_zip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
        file_ofs: u64,
        p_buf: *const c_void,
        n: usize,
    ) -> usize {
        let zip = &mut *p_zip;
        let state = &mut *zip.m_pState;
        
        if let Some(ref mut file) = state.m_pFile {
            if let Ok(_) = file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(file_ofs)) {
                let slice = std::slice::from_raw_parts(p_buf as *const u8, n);
                return file.write(slice).unwrap_or(0);
            }
        } else if !state.m_pMem.is_null() {
            let start = state.m_file_archive_start_ofs + file_ofs;
            let needed_size = (start + n as u64) as usize;
            
            if needed_size > state.m_mem_capacity {
                // Need to reallocate
                let new_capacity = needed_size.next_power_of_two();
                let new_ptr = (zip.m_pRealloc)(
                    zip.m_pAlloc_opaque,
                    state.m_pMem as *mut c_void,
                    new_capacity,
                ) as *mut u8;
                
                if new_ptr.is_null() {
                    return 0;
                }
                
                state.m_pMem = new_ptr;
                state.m_mem_capacity = new_capacity;
            }
            
            if needed_size > state.m_mem_size {
                state.m_mem_size = needed_size;
            }
            
            let dst = state.m_pMem.add(start as usize);
            let src = p_buf as *const u8;
            ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(src, dst, n);
            return n;
        }
        0
    }
}

#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Debug, Default, Clone, Copy)]
struct mz_zip_error {
    m_err: i32,
}

const MZ_ZIP_NO_ERROR: i32 = 0;
const MZ_ZIP_UNDEFINED_ERROR: i32 = -1;
const MZ_ZIP_TOO_MANY_FILES: i32 = -2;
const MZ_ZIP_FILE_TOO_LARGE: i32 = -3;
const MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_METHOD: i32 = -4;
const MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_ENCRYPTION: i32 = -5;
const MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE: i32 = -6;
const MZ_ZIP_FAILED_FINDING_CENTRAL_DIR: i32 = -7;
const MZ_ZIP_NOT_AN_ARCHIVE: i32 = -8;
const MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED: i32 = -9;
const MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_MULTIDISK: i32 = -10;
const MZ_ZIP_DECOMPRESSION_FAILED: i32 = -11;
const MZ_ZIP_COMPRESSION_FAILED: i32 = -12;
const MZ_ZIP_UNEXPECTED_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE: i32 = -13;
const MZ_ZIP_CRC_CHECK_FAILED: i32 = -14;
const MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_CDIR_SIZE: i32 = -15;
const MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED: i32 = -16;
const MZ_ZIP_FILE_OPEN_FAILED: i32 = -17;
const MZ_ZIP_FILE_CREATE_FAILED: i32 = -18;
const MZ_ZIP_FILE_WRITE_FAILED: i32 = -19;
const MZ_ZIP_FILE_READ_FAILED: i32 = -20;
const MZ_ZIP_FILE_CLOSE_FAILED: i32 = -21;
const MZ_ZIP_FILE_SEEK_FAILED: i32 = -22;
const MZ_ZIP_FILE_STAT_FAILED: i32 = -23;
const MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER: i32 = -24;
const MZ_ZIP_INVALID_FILENAME: i32 = -25;
const MZ_ZIP_BUF_TOO_SMALL: i32 = -26;
const MZ_ZIP_INTERNAL_ERROR: i32 = -27;
const MZ_ZIP_FILE_NOT_FOUND: i32 = -28;
const MZ_ZIP_ARCHIVE_TOO_LARGE: i32 = -29;
const MZ_ZIP_VALIDATION_FAILED: i32 = -30;
const MZ_ZIP_WRITE_CALLBACK_FAILED: i32 = -31;

fn mz_zip_set_error(p_zip: &mut mz_zip_archive, err: i32) -> bool {
    p_zip.m_last_error = err;
    false
}

fn mz_write_le16(p: &mut [u8], v: u16) {
    p[0] = v as u8;
    p[1] = (v >> 8) as u8;
}

fn mz_write_le32(p: &mut [u8], v: u32) {
    p[0] = v as u8;
    p[1] = (v >> 8) as u8;
    p[2] = (v >> 16) as u8;
    p[3] = (v >> 24) as u8;
}

fn mz_write_le64(p: &mut [u8], v: u64) {
    mz_write_le32(p, v as u32);
    mz_write_le32(&mut p[4..], (v >> 32) as u32);
}

fn mz_read_le16(p: &[u8]) -> u16 {
    p[0] as u16 | ((p[1] as u16) << 8)
}

fn mz_read_le32(p: &[u8]) -> u32 {
    p[0] as u32 | ((p[1] as u32) << 8) | ((p[2] as u32) << 16) | ((p[3] as u32) << 24)
}

fn mz_read_le64(p: &[u8]) -> u64 {
    let lo = mz_read_le32(p) as u64;
    let hi = mz_read_le32(&p[4..]) as u64;
    lo | (hi << 32)
}

fn mz_zip_writer_compute_padding_needed_for_file_alignment(p_zip: &mz_zip_archive) -> usize {
    let alignment = p_zip.m_file_offset_alignment;
    if alignment == 0 {
        return 0;
    }
    let cur_ofs = p_zip.m_archive_size;
    let padding = alignment - (cur_ofs % alignment as u64) as u32;
    if padding == alignment {
        0
    } else {
        padding as usize
    }
}

fn mz_zip_writer_write_zeros(p_zip: &mut mz_zip_archive, cur_dst_file_ofs: u64, num_bytes: usize) -> bool {
    if num_bytes == 0 {
        return true;
    }
    
    let zero_buf = vec![0u8; num_bytes.min(MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE)];
    let mut remaining = num_bytes;
    let mut offset = cur_dst_file_ofs;
    
    while remaining > 0 {
        let to_write = remaining.min(zero_buf.len());
        let written = (p_zip.m_pWrite)(p_zip as *mut mz_zip_archive, offset, zero_buf.as_ptr() as *const c_void, to_write);
        if written != to_write {
            return false;
        }
        offset += written as u64;
        remaining -= written;
    }
    
    true
}

fn mz_zip_get_cdh(p_zip: &mz_zip_archive, file_index: u32) -> Option<&[u8]> {
    unsafe {
        let state = &*p_zip.m_pState;
        if state.m_central_dir.m_size == 0 {
            return None;
        }
        
        let cdh_array = &state.m_central_dir_offsets;
        if file_index as usize >= cdh_array.m_size {
            return None;
        }
        
        let offset_ptr = state.m_central_dir_offsets.m_p.add(file_index as usize * 4);
        let offset = ptr::read_unaligned(offset_ptr as *const u32) as usize;
        
        if offset >= state.m_central_dir.m_size {
            return None;
        }
        
        let start = state.m_central_dir.m_p.add(offset);
        Some(slice::from_raw_parts(start, MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE))
    }
}

fn mz_zip_file_stat_internal(
    p_zip: &mz_zip_archive,
    file_index: u32,
    p_cd: &[u8],
    p_stat: &mut mz_zip_archive_file_stat,
    _p_src_needs_zip64: Option<&mut bool>,
) -> bool {
    if p_cd.len() < MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE {
        return false;
    }
    
    let sig = mz_read_le32(&p_cd[MZ_ZIP_CDH_SIG_OFS..]);
    if sig != MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
        return false;
    }
    
    let filename_len = mz_read_le16(&p_cd[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
    let extra_len = mz_read_le16(&p_cd[MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
    let comment_len = mz_read_le16(&p_cd[MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
    
    if p_cd.len() < MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_len + extra_len + comment_len {
        return false;
    }
    
    p_stat.m_file_index = file_index;
    p_stat.m_central_dir_ofs = 0; // Will be filled by caller if needed
    p_stat.m_version_made_by = mz_read_le16(&p_cd[4..]);
    p_stat.m_version_needed = mz_read_le16(&p_cd[6..]);
    p_stat.m_bit_flag = mz_read_le16(&p_cd[8..]);
    p_stat.m_method = mz_read_le16(&p_cd[10..]);
    p_stat.m_time = mz_read_le16(&p_cd[12..]) as u32;
    p_stat.m_date = mz_read_le16(&p_cd[14..]) as u32;
    p_stat.m_crc32 = mz_read_le32(&p_cd[16..]);
    
    let comp_size_32 = mz_read_le32(&p_cd[20..]);
    let uncomp_size_32 = mz_read_le32(&p_cd[24..]);
    
    p_stat.m_comp_size = comp_size_32 as u64;
    p_stat.m_uncomp_size = uncomp_size_32 as u64;
    p_stat.m_local_header_ofs = mz_read_le32(&p_cd[42..]) as u64;
    
    // Parse extra field for zip64 info
    if extra_len > 0 {
        let extra_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_len;
        let extra_data = &p_cd[extra_start..extra_start + extra_len];
        let mut pos = 0;
        
        while pos + 4 <= extra_len {
            let header_id = mz_read_le16(&extra_data[pos..]);
            let data_size = mz_read_le16(&extra_data[pos + 2..]) as usize;
            
            if pos + 4 + data_size > extra_len {
                break;
            }
            
            if header_id == MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID {
                let data = &extra_data[pos + 4..pos + 4 + data_size];
                let mut data_pos = 0;
                
                if uncomp_size_32 == MZ_UINT32_MAX as u32 && data_pos + 8 <= data_size {
                    p_stat.m_uncomp_size = mz_read_le64(&data[data_pos..]);
                    data_pos += 8;
                }
                
                if comp_size_32 == MZ_UINT32_MAX as u32 && data_pos + 8 <= data_size {
                    p_stat.m_comp_size = mz_read_le64(&data[data_pos..]);
                    data_pos += 8;
                }
                
                if p_stat.m_local_header_ofs == MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 && data_pos + 8 <= data_size {
                    p_stat.m_local_header_ofs = mz_read_le64(&data[data_pos..]);
                }
                
                break;
            }
            
            pos += 4 + data_size;
        }
    }
    
    // Extract filename
    let filename_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE;
    let filename_bytes = &p_cd[filename_start..filename_start + filename_len];
    p_stat.m_archive_name = String::from_utf8_lossy(filename_bytes).to_string();
    
    // Extract comment
    let comment_start = filename_start + filename_len + extra_len;
    let comment_bytes = &p_cd[comment_start..comment_start + comment_len];
    p_stat.m_comment = String::from_utf8_lossy(comment_bytes).to_string();
    
    p_stat.m_is_directory = p_stat.m_archive_name.ends_with('/');
    p_stat.m_is_encrypted = (p_stat.m_bit_flag & 1) != 0;
    p_stat.m_is_supported = p_stat.m_method == 0 || p_stat.m_method == 8; // Store or deflate
    
    true
}

fn mz_zip_writer_update_zip64_extension_block(
    new_ext_block: &mut mz_zip_array,
    p_zip: &mut mz_zip_archive,
    p_src_ext: *const u8,
    src_ext_len: usize,
    p_comp_size: &u64,
    p_uncomp_size: &u64,
    p_local_header_ofs: &u64,
    _p_disk_start: Option<&u32>,
) -> bool {
    let mut buf = Vec::with_capacity(64);
    
    // Write zip64 extended information field header
    mz_write_le16(&mut buf, MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID);
    
    let mut data_size = 0;
    let mut field_size_pos = buf.len();
    buf.extend_from_slice(&[0u8; 2]); // Placeholder for data size
    
    let src_needs_zip64 = *p_comp_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 
        || *p_uncomp_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 
        || *p_local_header_ofs >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64;
    
    if src_needs_zip64 {
        mz_write_le64(&mut buf, *p_uncomp_size);
        mz_write_le64(&mut buf, *p_comp_size);
        mz_write_le64(&mut buf, *p_local_header_ofs);
        data_size = 24;
    } else {
        // Still need to write the field even if values fit in 32-bit?
        // According to ZIP spec, zip64 extended info should only be present when needed
        // But miniz seems to handle this differently
        data_size = 0;
    }
    
    // Update data size field
    mz_write_le16(&mut buf[field_size_pos..], data_size as u16);
    
    new_ext_block.clear(p_zip);
    new_ext_block.push_back(p_zip, &buf)
}

fn mz_zip_writer_add_from_zip_reader(
    p_zip: &mut mz_zip_archive,
    p_source_zip: &mut mz_zip_archive,
    src_file_index: u32,
) -> bool {
    // Safety: We're accessing the internal state through raw pointers
    let p_state = unsafe { &mut *p_zip.m_pState };
    let src_state = unsafe { &*p_source_zip.m_pState };
    
    // Sanity checks
    if p_zip.m_pState.is_null() || p_zip.m_zip_mode != 2 /* MZ_ZIP_MODE_WRITING */
        || p_source_zip.m_pRead.is_null()
    {
        return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
    }
    
    // Don't support copying files from zip64 archives to non-zip64
    if src_state.m_zip64 && !p_state.m_zip64 {
        return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
    }
    
    // Get pointer to the source central dir header
    let p_src_central_header = match mz_zip_get_cdh(p_source_zip, src_file_index) {
        Some(hdr) => hdr,
        None => return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER),
    };
    
    if mz_read_le32(&p_src_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_SIG_OFS..]) != MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
        return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
    }
    
    let src_filename_len = mz_read_le16(&p_src_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
    let src_comment_len = mz_read_le16(&p_src_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
    let src_ext_len = mz_read_le16(&p_src_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
    let src_central_dir_following_data_size = src_filename_len + src_ext_len + src_comment_len;
    
    // Check central directory size limits
    if (p_state.m_central_dir.m_size + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_central_dir_following_data_size + 32) >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as usize {
        return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_CDIR_SIZE);
    }
    
    let num_alignment_padding_bytes = mz_zip_writer_compute_padding_needed_for_file_alignment(p_zip);
    
    if !p_state.m_zip64 {
        if p_zip.m_total_files == MZ_UINT16_MAX {
            return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_TOO_MANY_FILES);
        }
    } else if p_zip.m_total_files == MZ_UINT32_MAX {
        return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_TOO_MANY_FILES);
    }
    
    let mut src_file_stat = mz_zip_archive_file_stat::default();
    if !mz_zip_file_stat_internal(p_source_zip, src_file_index, p_src_central_header, &mut src_file_stat, None) {
        return false;
    }
    
    let mut cur_src_file_ofs = src_file_stat.m_local_header_ofs;
    let mut cur_dst_file_ofs = p_zip.m_archive_size;
    
    // Read the source archive's local dir header
    let mut local_header = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
    let read_result = (p_source_zip.m_pRead)(
        p_source_zip as *const _ as *mut mz_zip_archive,
        cur_src_file_ofs,
        local_header.as_mut_ptr() as *mut c_void,
        MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE,
    );
    
    if read_result != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE {
        return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_READ_FAILED);
    }
    
    if mz_read_le32(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_SIG_OFS..]) != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
        return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
    }
    
    cur_src_file_ofs += MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64;
    
    let local_header_filename_size = mz_read_le16(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
    let local_header_extra_len = mz_read_le16(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
    let mut local_header_comp_size = mz_read_le32(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..]) as u64;
    let mut local_header_uncomp_size = mz_read_le32(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..]) as u64;
    
    let mut src_archive_bytes_remaining = src_file_stat.m_comp_size + local_header_filename_size as u64 + local_header_extra_len as u64;
    let mut found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir = false;
    
    // Try to find zip64 extended information field in local header
    if local_header_extra_len > 0 && (local_header_comp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX || local_header_uncomp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX) {
        let mut file_data_array = mz_zip_array::new(1);
        if !file_data_array.resize(p_zip, local_header_extra_len, false) {
            return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED);
        }
        
        let read_result = (p_source_zip.m_pRead)(
            p_source_zip as *const _ as *mut mz_zip_archive,
            src_file_stat.m_local_header_ofs + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 + local_header_filename_size as u64,
            file_data_array.m_p as *mut c_void,
            local_header_extra_len,
        );
        
        if read_result != local_header_extra_len {
            file_data_array.clear(p_zip);
            return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_READ_FAILED);
        }
        
        let p_extra_data = unsafe { slice::from_raw_parts(file_data_array.m_p, local_header_extra_len) };
        let mut extra_size_remaining = local_header_extra_len;
        let mut pos = 0;
        
        while extra_size_remaining >= 4 {
            let field_id = mz_read_le16(&p_extra_data[pos..]);
            let field_data_size = mz_read_le16(&p_extra_data[pos + 2..]) as usize;
            let field_total_size = field_data_size + 4;
            
            if field_total_size > extra_size_remaining {
                file_data_array.clear(p_zip);
                return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
            }
            
            if field_id == MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID {
                if field_data_size < 16 {
                    file_data_array.clear(p_zip);
                    return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
                }
                
                let field_data = &p_extra_data[pos + 4..pos + 4 + field_data_size];
                local_header_uncomp_size = mz_read_le64(&field_data[0..8]);
                local_header_comp_size = mz_read_le64(&field_data[8..16]);
                found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir = true;
                break;
            }
            
            pos += field_total_size;
            extra_size_remaining -= field_total_size;
        }
        
        file_data_array.clear(p_zip);
    }
    
    // Check archive size limits for non-zip64
    if !p_state.m_zip64 {
        let approx_new_archive_size = cur_dst_file_ofs
            + num_alignment_padding_bytes as u64
            + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64
            + src_archive_bytes_remaining
            + 16 // sizeof(mz_uint32) * 4
            + p_state.m_central_dir.m_size as u64
            + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64
            + src_central_dir_following_data_size as u64
            + MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64
            + 64;
        
        if approx_new_archive_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX {
            return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_ARCHIVE_TOO_LARGE);
        }
    }
    
    // Write dest archive padding
    if !mz_zip_writer_write_zeros(p_zip, cur_dst_file_ofs, num_alignment_padding_bytes) {
        return false;
    }
    
    cur_dst_file_ofs += num_alignment_padding_bytes as u64;
    let local_dir_header_ofs = cur_dst_file_ofs;
    
    // Copy local header
    let write_result = (p_zip.m_pWrite)(
        p_zip as *mut mz_zip_archive,
        cur_dst_file_ofs,
        local_header.as_ptr() as *const c_void,
        MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE,
    );
    
    if write_result != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE {
        return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_WRITE_FAILED);
    }
    
    cur_dst_file_ofs += MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64;
    
    // Allocate buffer for copying file data
    let buf_size = (MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE as u64).min(src_archive_bytes_remaining).max(32) as usize;
    let p_buf = (p_zip.m_pAlloc)(p_zip.m_pAlloc_opaque, 1, buf_size);
    if p_buf.is_null() {
        return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED);
    }
    
    // Copy file data
    while src_archive_bytes_remaining > 0 {
        let to_read = (MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE as u64).min(src_archive_bytes_remaining) as usize;
        let read = (p_source_zip.m_pRead)(
            p_source_zip as *const _ as *mut mz_zip_archive,
            cur_src_file_ofs,
            p_buf,
            to_read,
        );
        
        if read != to_read {
            (p_zip.m_pFree)(p_zip.m_pAlloc_opaque, p_buf);
            return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_READ_FAILED);
        }
        
        cur_src_file_ofs += read as u64;
        
        let written = (p_zip.m_pWrite)(
            p_zip as *mut mz_zip_archive,
            cur_dst_file_ofs,
            p_buf,
            read,
        );
        
        if written != read {
            (p_zip.m_pFree)(p_zip.m_pAlloc_opaque, p_buf);
            return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_WRITE_FAILED);
        }
        
        cur_dst_file_ofs += written as u64;
        src_archive_bytes_remaining -= written as u64;
    }
    
    // Handle data descriptor if present
    let bit_flags = mz_read_le16(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS..]);
    if (bit_flags & 8) != 0 {
        let mut data_desc_buf = [0u8; 24];
        
        if src_state.m_zip64 || found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir {
            // Read zip64 data descriptor
            let read = (p_source_zip.m_pRead)(
                p_source_zip as *const _ as *mut mz_zip_archive,
                cur_src_file_ofs,
                data_desc_buf.as_mut_ptr() as *mut c_void,
                24,
            );
            
            if read != 24 {
                (p_zip.m_pFree)(p_zip.m_pAlloc_opaque, p_buf);
                return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_READ_FAILED);
            }
            
            let n = if mz_read_le32(&data_desc_buf[0..4]) == MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTOR_ID {
                24
            } else {
                20
            };
            
            let written = (p_zip.m_pWrite)(
                p_zip as *mut mz_zip_archive,
                cur_dst_file_ofs,
                data_desc_buf.as_ptr() as *const c_void,
                n,
            );
            
            if written != n {
                (p_zip.m_pFree)(p_zip.m_pAlloc_opaque, p_buf);
                return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_WRITE_FAILED);
            }
            
            cur_src_file_ofs += n as u64;
            cur_dst_file_ofs += n as u64;
        } else {
            // Read non-zip64 data descriptor
            let mut temp_buf = [0u8; 16];
            let read = (p_source_zip.m_pRead)(
                p_source_zip as *const _ as *mut mz_zip_archive,
                cur_src_file_ofs,
                temp_buf.as_mut_ptr() as *mut c_void,
                16,
            );
            
            if read != 16 {
                (p_zip.m_pFree)(p_zip.m_pAlloc_opaque, p_buf);
                return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_READ_FAILED);
            }
            
            let has_id = mz_read_le32(&temp_buf[0..4]) == MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTOR_ID;
            
            if p_state.m_zip64 {
                // Upgrade to zip64 data descriptor
                let src_offset = if has_id { 4 } else { 0 };
                let src_crc32 = mz_read_le32(&temp_buf[src_offset..src_offset + 4]);
                let src_comp_size = mz_read_le32(&temp_buf[src_offset + 4..src_offset + 8]) as u64;
                let src_uncomp_size = mz_read_le32(&temp_buf[src_offset + 8..src_offset + 12]) as u64;
                
                mz_write_le32(&mut data_desc_buf[0..4], MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTOR_ID);
                mz_write_le32(&mut data_desc_buf[4..8], src_crc32);
                mz_write_le64(&mut data_desc_buf[8..16], src_comp_size);
                mz_write_le64(&mut data_desc_buf[16..24], src_uncomp_size);
                
                let written = (p_zip.m_pWrite)(
                    p_zip as *mut mz_zip_archive,
                    cur_dst_file_ofs,
                    data_desc_buf.as_ptr() as *const c_void,
                    24,
                );
                
                if written != 24 {
                    (p_zip.m_pFree)(p_zip.m_pAlloc_opaque, p_buf);
                    return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_WRITE_FAILED);
                }
                
                cur_src_file_ofs += (if has_id { 16 } else { 12 }) as u64;
                cur_dst_file_ofs += 24;
            } else {
                // Copy as-is
                let n = if has_id { 16 } else { 12 };
                let written = (p_zip.m_pWrite)(
                    p_zip as *mut mz_zip_archive,
                    cur_dst_file_ofs,
                    temp_buf.as_ptr() as *const c_void,
                    n,
                );
                
                if written != n {
                    (p_zip.m_pFree)(p_zip.m_pAlloc_opaque, p_buf);
                    return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_WRITE_FAILED);
                }
                
                cur_src_file_ofs += n as u64;
                cur_dst_file_ofs += n as u64;
            }
        }
    }
    
    (p_zip.m_pFree)(p_zip.m_pAlloc_opaque, p_buf);
    
    // Add new central directory entry
    let orig_central_dir_size = p_state.m_central_dir.m_size;
    let mut new_central_header = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
    new_central_header.copy_from_slice(&p_src_central_header[0..MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE]);
    
    if p_state.m_zip64 {
        // Create new zip64 extension block
        let mut new_ext_block = mz_zip_array::new(1);
        let p_src_ext = &p_src_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_filename_len..];
        
        if !mz_zip_writer_update_zip64_extension_block(
            &mut new_ext_block,
            p_zip,
            p_src_ext.as_ptr(),
            src_ext_len,
            &src_file_stat.m_comp_size,
            &src_file_stat.m_uncomp_size,
            &local_dir_header_ofs,
            None,
        ) {
            new_ext_block.clear(p_zip);
            return false;
        }
        
        // Update central header for zip64
        mz_write_le32(&mut new_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..], MZ_UINT32_MAX as u32);
        mz_write_le32(&mut new_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..], MZ_UINT32_MAX as u32);
        mz_write_le32(&mut new_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS..], MZ_UINT32_MAX as u32);
        mz_write_le16(&mut new_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..], new_ext_block.m_size as u16);
        
        // Push central header
        if !p_state.m_central_dir.push_back(p_zip, &new_central_header) {
            new_ext_block.clear(p_zip);
            return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED);
        }
        
        // Push filename
        let filename_slice = &p_src_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE..MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_filename_len];
        if !p_state.m_central_dir.push_back(p_zip, filename_slice) {
            new_ext_block.clear(p_zip);
            p_state.m_central_dir.resize(p_zip, orig_central_dir_size, false);
            return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED);
        }
        
        // Push extension block
        unsafe {
            let ext_slice = slice::from_raw_parts(new_ext_block.m_p, new_ext_block.m_size);
            if !p_state.m_central_dir.push_back(p_zip, ext_slice) {
                new_ext_block.clear(p_zip);
                p_state.m_central_dir.resize(p_zip, orig_central_dir_size, false);
                return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED);
            }
        }
        
        // Push comment
        let comment_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_filename_len + src_ext_len;
        let comment_slice = &p_src_central_header[comment_start..comment_start + src_comment_len];
        if !p_state.m_central_dir.push_back(p_zip, comment_slice) {
            new_ext_block.clear(p_zip);
            p_state.m_central_dir.resize(p_zip, orig_central_dir_size, false);
            return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED);
        }
        
        new_ext_block.clear(p_zip);
    } else {
        // Non-zip64 mode
        if cur_dst_file_ofs > MZ_UINT32_MAX || local_dir_header_ofs >= MZ_UINT32_MAX {
            return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_ARCHIVE_TOO_LARGE);
        }
        
        mz_write_le32(&mut new_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS..], local_dir_header_ofs as u32);
        
        if !p_state.m_central_dir.push_back(p_zip, &new_central_header) {
            return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED);
        }
        
        let following_data = &p_src_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE..MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_central_dir_following_data_size];
        if !p_state.m_central_dir.push_back(p_zip, following_data) {
            p_state.m_central_dir.resize(p_zip, orig_central_dir_size, false);
            return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED);
        }
    }
    
    // Check central directory size
    if p_state.m_central_dir.m_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as usize {
        p_state.m_central_dir.resize(p_zip, orig_central_dir_size, false);
        return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_CDIR_SIZE);
    }
    
    // Add offset to central directory offsets array
    let offset_val = orig_central_dir_size as u32;
    let offset_bytes = offset_val.to_le_bytes();
    if !p_state.m_central_dir_offsets.push_back(p_zip, &offset_bytes) {
        p_state.m_central_dir.resize(p_zip, orig_central_dir_size, false);
        return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED);
    }
    
    p_zip.m_total_files += 1;
    p_zip.m_archive_size = cur_dst_file_ofs;
    
    true
}

fn mz_zip_writer_finalize_archive(p_zip: &mut mz_zip_archive) -> bool {
    let p_state = unsafe { &mut *p_zip.m_pState };
    
    if p_zip.m_pState.is_null() || p_zip.m_zip_mode != 2 /* MZ_ZIP_MODE_WRITING */ {
        return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
    }
    
    if p_state.m_zip64 {
        if p_state.m_central_dir.m_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as usize {
            return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_TOO_MANY_FILES);
        }
    } else {
        if p_zip.m_total_files > MZ_UINT16_MAX as u32
            || (p_zip.m_archive_size + p_state.m_central_dir.m_size as u64 + MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64) > MZ_UINT32_MAX
        {
            return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_TOO_MANY_FILES);
        }
    }
    
    let mut central_dir_ofs = 0u64;
    let mut central_dir_size = 0u64;
    
    if p_zip.m_total_files > 0 {
        central_dir_ofs = p_zip.m_archive_size;
        central_dir_size = p_state.m_central_dir.m_size as u64;
        p_zip.m_central_directory_file_ofs = central_dir_ofs;
        
        let written = (p_zip.m_pWrite)(
            p_zip as *mut mz_zip_archive,
            central_dir_ofs,
            p_state.m_central_dir.m_p as *const c_void,
            p_state.m_central_dir.m_size,
        );
        
        if written != p_state.m_central_dir.m_size {
            return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_WRITE_FAILED);
        }
        
        p_zip.m_archive_size += central_dir_size;
    }
    
    let mut hdr = [0u8; 256];
    
    if p_state.m_zip64 {
        // Write zip64 end of central directory header
        let rel_ofs_to_zip64_ecdr = p_zip.m_archive_size;
        
        mz_write_le32(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_SIG_OFS..], MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG);
        mz_write_le64(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_SIZE_OF_RECORD_OFS..], (MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE - 12) as u64); // - sig(4) - size(8)
        mz_write_le16(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_VERSION_MADE_BY_OFS..], 0x031E); // Unix
        mz_write_le16(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_VERSION_NEEDED_OFS..], 0x002D); // 4.5
        mz_write_le64(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_NUM_ENTRIES_ON_DISK_OFS..], p_zip.m_total_files as u64);
        mz_write_le64(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_TOTAL_ENTRIES_OFS..], p_zip.m_total_files as u64);
        mz_write_le64(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_SIZE_OFS..], central_dir_size);
        mz_write_le64(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS..], central_dir_ofs);
        
        let written = (p_zip.m_pWrite)(
            p_zip as *mut mz_zip_archive,
            p_zip.m_archive_size,
            hdr.as_ptr() as *const c_void,
            MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE,
        );
        
        if written != MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE {
            return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_WRITE_FAILED);
        }
        
        p_zip.m_archive_size += MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64;
        
        // Write zip64 end of central directory locator
        hdr = [0u8; 256];
        mz_write_le32(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_SIG_OFS..], MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIG);
        mz_write_le64(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_REL_OFS_TO_ZIP64_ECDR_OFS..], rel_ofs_to_zip64_ecdr);
        mz_write_le32(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_TOTAL_NUMBER_OF_DISKS_OFS..], 1);
        
        let written = (p_zip.m_pWrite)(
            p_zip as *mut mz_zip_archive,
            p_zip.m_archive_size,
            hdr.as_ptr() as *const c_void,
            MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE,
        );
        
        if written != MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE {
            return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_WRITE_FAILED);
        }
        
        p_zip.m_archive_size += MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE as u64;
    }
    
    // Write end of central directory record
    hdr = [0u8; 256];
    mz_write_le32(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_SIG_OFS..], MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG);
    mz_write_le16(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_NUM_ENTRIES_ON_DISK_OFS..], (p_zip.m_total_files.min(MZ_UINT16_MAX as u32)) as u16);
    mz_write_le16(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_TOTAL_ENTRIES_OFS..], (p_zip.m_total_files.min(MZ_UINT16_MAX as u32)) as u16);
    mz_write_le32(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_SIZE_OFS..], central_dir_size.min(MZ_UINT32_MAX) as u32);
    mz_write_le32(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS..], central_dir_ofs.min(MZ_UINT32_MAX) as u32);
    
    let written = (p_zip.m_pWrite)(
        p_zip as *mut mz_zip_archive,
        p_zip.m_archive_size,
        hdr.as_ptr() as *const c_void,
        MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE,
    );
    
    if written != MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE {
        return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_WRITE_FAILED);
    }
    
    p_zip.m_archive_size += MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64;
    
    // Flush file if we have one
    if let Some(ref mut file) = p_state.m_pFile {
        if let Err(_) = file.flush() {
            return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_CLOSE_FAILED);
        }
    }
    
    p_zip.m_zip_mode = 3; // MZ_ZIP_MODE_WRITING_HAS_BEEN_FINALIZED
    true
}

fn mz_zip_heap_write_func(
    _p_zip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    _file_ofs: u64,
    _p_buf: *const c_void,
    _n: usize,
) -> usize {
    // This is a placeholder - the actual implementation would write to heap
    0
}

fn mz_zip_writer_finalize_heap_archive(
    p_zip: &mut mz_zip_archive,
    pp_buf: &mut *mut c_void,
    p_size: &mut usize,
) -> bool {
    if pp_buf.is_null() || p_size.is_null() {
        return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
    }
    
    *pp_buf = ptr::null_mut();
    *p_size = 0;
    
    if p_zip.m_pState.is_null() {
        return mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
    }
    
    // Note: We can't compare function pointers directly in safe Rust
    // This check is skipped for now
    
    if !mz_zip_writer_finalize_archive(p_zip) {
        return false;
    }
    
    let p_state = unsafe { &mut *p_zip.m_pState };
    *pp_buf = p_state.m_pMem as *mut c_void;
    *p_size = p_state.m_mem_size;
    p_state.m_pMem = ptr::null_mut();
    p_state.m_mem_size = 0;
    p_state.m_mem_capacity = 0;
    
    true
}

fn mz_zip_writer_end_internal(_p_zip: *mut mz_zip_archive, _preserve: bool) -> bool {
    // Placeholder implementation
    true
}

fn mz_zip_writer_end(p_zip: &mut mz_zip_archive) -> bool {
    mz_zip_writer_end_internal(p_zip as *mut mz_zip_archive, true)
}

fn mz_zip_writer_validate_archive_name(_archive_name: &str) -> bool {
    // Basic validation: no null bytes, reasonable length
    !_archive_name.contains('\0') && _archive_name.len() <= 0xFFFF
}

fn mz_zip_add_mem_to_archive_file_in_place(
    p_zip_filename: &str,
    p_archive_name: &str,
    p_buf: *const c_void,
    buf_size: usize,
    p_comment: *const c_void,
    comment_size: u16,
    level_and_flags: u32,
) -> bool {
    mz_zip_add_mem_to_archive_file_in_place_v2(
        p_zip_filename,
        p_archive_name,
        p_buf,
        buf_size,
        p_comment,
        comment_size,
        level_and_flags,
        ptr::null_mut(),
    )
}

fn mz_zip_add_mem_to_archive_file_in_place_v2(
    p_zip_filename: &str,
    p_archive_name: &str,
    p_buf: *const c_void,
    buf_size: usize,
    p_comment: *const c_void,
    comment_size: u16,
    level_and_flags: u32,
    p_err: *mut mz_zip_error,
) -> bool {
    let mut status = false;
    let mut created_new_archive = false;
    let mut zip_archive = mz_zip_archive::new();
    let mut actual_err = MZ_ZIP_NO_ERROR;
    
    if (level_and_flags as i32) < 0 {
        // level_and_flags would be set to MZ_DEFAULT_LEVEL
    }
    
    if p_zip_filename.is_empty() 
        || p_archive_name.is_empty() 
        || (buf_size > 0 && p_buf.is_null()) 
        || (comment_size > 0 && p_comment.is_null()) 
        || ((level_and_flags & 0xF) > 10) // MZ_UBER_COMPRESSION
    {
        if !p_err.is_null() {
            unsafe { (*p_err).m_err = MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER };
        }
        return false;
    }
    
    if !mz_zip_writer_validate_archive_name(p_archive_name) {
        if !p_err.is_null() {
            unsafe { (*p_err).m_err = MZ_ZIP_INVALID_FILENAME };
        }
        return false;
    }
    
    // Check if file exists
    let file_stat = fs::metadata(p_zip_filename);
    match file_stat {
        Ok(_) => {
            // File exists, append to it
            if !mz_zip_reader_init_file_v2(
                &mut zip_archive,
                p_zip_filename,
                level_and_flags | 0x2000 | 0x1000, // MZ_ZIP_FLAG_DO_NOT_SORT_CENTRAL_DIRECTORY | MZ_ZIP_FLAG_READ_ALLOW_WRITING
                0,
                0,
            ) {
                if !p_err.is_null() {
                    unsafe { (*p_err).m_err = zip_archive.m_last_error };
                }
                return false;
            }
            
            if !mz_zip_writer_init_from_reader_v2(
                &mut zip_archive,
                p_zip_filename,
                level_and_flags | 0x1000, // MZ_ZIP_FLAG_READ_ALLOW_WRITING
            ) {
                if !p_err.is_null() {
                    unsafe { (*p_err).m_err = zip_archive.m_last_error };
                }
                
                mz_zip_reader_end_internal(&mut zip_archive, false);
                return false;
            }
        }
        Err(_) => {
            // Create new archive
            if !mz_zip_writer_init_file_v2(&mut zip_archive, p_zip_filename, 0, level_and_flags) {
                if !p_err.is_null() {
                    unsafe { (*p_err).m_err = zip_archive.m_last_error };
                }
                return false;
            }
            created_new_archive = true;
        }
    }
    
    status = mz_zip_writer_add_mem_ex(
        &mut zip_archive,
        p_archive_name,
        p_buf,
        buf_size,
        p_comment,
        comment_size,
        level_and_flags,
        None,
        ptr::null_mut(),
    );
    
    actual_err = zip_archive.m_last_error;
    
    // Always try to finalize
    if !mz_zip_writer_finalize_archive(&mut zip_archive) {
        if actual_err == MZ_ZIP_NO_ERROR {
            actual_err = zip_archive.m_last_error;
        }
        status = false;
    }
    
    if !mz_zip_writer_end_internal(&mut zip_archive as *mut _, status) {
        if actual_err == MZ_ZIP_NO_ERROR {
            actual_err = zip_archive.m_last_error;
        }
        status = false;
    }
    
    if !status && created_new_archive {
        // Delete the failed new archive
        let _ = fs::remove_file(p_zip_filename);
    }
    
    if !p_err.is_null() {
        unsafe { (*p_err).m_err = actual_err };
    }
    
    status
}

// --- Module: mz_p9 ---
// Helper function to check if pointer is NULL
fn ptr_is_null<T>(ptr: *const T) -> bool {
    ptr.is_null()
}

// Helper for conditional compilation - we'll use std::fs instead
#[cfg(not(feature = "no_std_io"))]
fn open_zip_file(path: &str, flags: u32) -> Result<std::fs::File, MzZipError> {
    std::fs::File::open(path).map_err(|_| MzZipError::FileOpenFailed)
}

pub fn mz_zip_extract_archive_file_to_heap_v2(
    pZip_filename: &str,
    pArchive_name: &str,
    pComment: Option<&str>,
    pSize: &mut usize,
    flags: u32,
    pErr: &mut Option<MzZipError>,
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MzZipError> {
    *pSize = 0;
    
    if pZip_filename.is_empty() || pArchive_name.is_empty() {
        if let Some(err) = pErr {
            *err = MzZipError::InvalidParameter;
        }
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    // Initialize zip archive
    let mut zip_archive = MzZipArchive::default();
    
    // Initialize file reader
    #[cfg(not(feature = "no_std_io"))]
    {
        let file = open_zip_file(pZip_filename, flags | MZ_ZIP_FLAG_DO_NOT_SORT_CENTRAL_DIRECTORY)?;
        // Note: This assumes mz_zip_reader_init_file_v2 exists in Rust
        // We'll need to implement it separately
        if let Err(e) = mz_zip_reader_init_file_v2(&mut zip_archive, &file, flags, 0, 0) {
            if let Some(err) = pErr {
                *err = zip_archive.m_last_error;
            }
            return Err(e);
        }
    }
    
    #[cfg(feature = "no_std_io")]
    {
        return Err(MzZipError::UnsupportedFeature);
    }
    
    // Locate file
    let file_index = if let Some(comment) = pComment {
        mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(&zip_archive, pArchive_name, Some(comment), flags)
    } else {
        mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(&zip_archive, pArchive_name, None, flags)
    };
    
    let result = if let Some(index) = file_index {
        mz_zip_reader_extract_to_heap(&zip_archive, index, flags)
    } else {
        Err(MzZipError::FileNotFound)
    };
    
    // Clean up
    mz_zip_reader_end_internal(&mut zip_archive, result.is_ok());
    
    if let Some(err) = pErr {
        *err = zip_archive.m_last_error;
    }
    
    result.map(|data| {
        *pSize = data.len();
        data
    })
}

pub fn mz_zip_extract_archive_file_to_heap(
    pZip_filename: &str,
    pArchive_name: &str,
    pSize: &mut usize,
    flags: u32,
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MzZipError> {
    mz_zip_extract_archive_file_to_heap_v2(pZip_filename, pArchive_name, None, pSize, flags, &mut None)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_mode(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> MzZipMode {
    pZip.map_or(MzZipMode::Invalid, |zip| zip.m_zip_mode)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_type(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> MzZipType {
    pZip.map_or(MzZipType::Invalid, |zip| zip.m_zip_type)
}

pub fn mz_zip_set_last_error(pZip: &mut MzZipArchive, err_num: MzZipError) -> MzZipError {
    let prev_err = pZip.m_last_error;
    pZip.m_last_error = err_num;
    prev_err
}

pub fn mz_zip_peek_last_error(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> MzZipError {
    pZip.map_or(MzZipError::InvalidParameter, |zip| zip.m_last_error)
}

pub fn mz_zip_clear_last_error(pZip: &mut MzZipArchive) -> MzZipError {
    mz_zip_set_last_error(pZip, MzZipError::NoError)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_last_error(pZip: &mut MzZipArchive) -> MzZipError {
    let prev_err = pZip.m_last_error;
    pZip.m_last_error = MzZipError::NoError;
    prev_err
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_error_string(mz_err: MzZipError) -> &'static str {
    match mz_err {
        MzZipError::NoError => "no error",
        MzZipError::UndefinedError => "undefined error",
        MzZipError::TooManyFiles => "too many files",
        MzZipError::FileTooLarge => "file too large",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedMethod => "unsupported method",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedEncryption => "unsupported encryption",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedFeature => "unsupported feature",
        MzZipError::FailedFindingCentralDir => "failed finding central directory",
        MzZipError::NotAnArchive => "not a ZIP archive",
        MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted => "invalid header or archive is corrupted",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedMultidisk => "unsupported multidisk archive",
        MzZipError::DecompressionFailed => "decompression failed or archive is corrupted",
        MzZipError::CompressionFailed => "compression failed",
        MzZipError::UnexpectedDecompressedSize => "unexpected decompressed size",
        MzZipError::CrcCheckFailed => "CRC-32 check failed",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedCdirSize => "unsupported central directory size",
        MzZipError::AllocFailed => "allocation failed",
        MzZipError::FileOpenFailed => "file open failed",
        MzZipError::FileCreateFailed => "file create failed",
        MzZipError::FileWriteFailed => "file write failed",
        MzZipError::FileReadFailed => "file read failed",
        MzZipError::FileCloseFailed => "file close failed",
        MzZipError::FileSeekFailed => "file seek failed",
        MzZipError::FileStatFailed => "file stat failed",
        MzZipError::InvalidParameter => "invalid parameter",
        MzZipError::InvalidFilename => "invalid filename",
        MzZipError::BufTooSmall => "buffer too small",
        MzZipError::InternalError => "internal error",
        MzZipError::FileNotFound => "file not found",
        MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge => "archive is too large",
        MzZipError::ValidationFailed => "validation failed",
        MzZipError::WriteCallbackFailed => "write callback failed",
        MzZipError::TotalErrors => "total errors",
        // Note: Some enum variants might not be in our Rust version
        // We'll handle the ones that are defined
        _ => "unknown error",
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_is_zip64(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> bool {
    pZip.and_then(|zip| zip.m_pstate.as_ref())
        .map_or(false, |state| state.zip64)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_central_dir_size(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> usize {
    pZip.and_then(|zip| zip.m_pstate.as_ref())
        .map_or(0, |state| state.central_dir.size)
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_get_num_files(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> u32 {
    pZip.map_or(0, |zip| zip.m_total_files)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_archive_size(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> u64 {
    pZip.map_or(0, |zip| zip.m_archive_size)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_archive_file_start_offset(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> u64 {
    pZip.and_then(|zip| zip.m_pstate.as_ref())
        .map_or(0, |state| state.file_archive_start_ofs)
}

// Note: MZ_FILE is typically std::fs::File in Rust
pub fn mz_zip_get_cfile(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> Option<&std::fs::File> {
    pZip.and_then(|zip| zip.m_pstate.as_ref())
        .and_then(|state| state.pfile.as_ref())
}

pub fn mz_zip_read_archive_data(
    pZip: &MzZipArchive,
    file_ofs: u64,
    pBuf: &mut [u8],
) -> Result<usize, MzZipError> {
    if pZip.m_pstate.is_none() || pBuf.is_empty() || pZip.m_pread.is_none() {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    if let Some(read_fn) = pZip.m_pread {
        let n = read_fn(pZip.m_pio_opaque.as_ref(), file_ofs, pBuf);
        Ok(n)
    } else {
        Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter)
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_get_filename(
    pZip: &MzZipArchive,
    file_index: u32,
    pFilename: &mut [u8],
) -> Result<u32, MzZipError> {
    // Get central directory header
    let cdh = mz_zip_get_cdh(pZip, file_index);
    if cdh.is_none() {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        if !pFilename.is_empty() {
            pFilename[0] = 0;
        }
        return Ok(0);
    }
    
    let cdh_slice = cdh.unwrap();
    
    // Read filename length from header
    if cdh_slice.len() < MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as usize + 2 {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
        return Ok(0);
    }
    
    let filename_len = u16::from_le_bytes([
        cdh_slice[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as usize],
        cdh_slice[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as usize + 1],
    ]) as usize;
    
    let filename_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as usize;
    
    // Copy filename to buffer if buffer is provided
    if !pFilename.is_empty() {
        let copy_len = filename_len.min(pFilename.len() - 1);
        
        if cdh_slice.len() >= filename_start + copy_len {
            pFilename[..copy_len].copy_from_slice(
                &cdh_slice[filename_start..filename_start + copy_len],
            );
        }
        pFilename[copy_len] = 0;
    }
    
    // Return required buffer size (including null terminator)
    Ok((filename_len + 1) as u32)
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_file_stat(
    pZip: &MzZipArchive,
    file_index: u32,
    pStat: &mut MzZipArchiveFileStat,
) -> bool {
    let cdh = mz_zip_get_cdh(pZip, file_index);
    mz_zip_file_stat_internal(pZip, file_index, cdh, pStat)
}

pub fn mz_zip_end(pZip: &mut MzZipArchive) -> bool {
    match pZip.m_zip_mode {
        MzZipMode::Reading => mz_zip_reader_end(pZip),
        MzZipMode::Writing | MzZipMode::WritingHasBeenFinalized => {
            #[cfg(feature = "archive_writing")]
            {
                mz_zip_writer_end(pZip)
            }
            #[cfg(not(feature = "archive_writing"))]
            {
                false
            }
        }
        _ => false,
    }
}
